class Component extends DCLogic {
  constructor(p){
    super(p);
    const prof = {}, essProf = {};
    const wcls = {};
    CAT.forEach(c => { const r = hash(c.id+'w');
      wcls[c.id] = {cat: r%7===0 ? '' : WCATS0[r % WCATS0.length],
        tags: WTAGS0.filter((t,i)=>((r >> i) & 1) === 1 && i < 4).slice(0,2)}; });
    CAT.forEach(c => SYS.forEach(s => {
      const r = hash(c.id+s.id), v = VOCAB[s.v];
      if (r % 10 < 4) return;
      const tagCount = r % 3;
      const ess = {};
      v.ess.forEach((k,i) => { if ((r >> (i+2)) % 3 === 0) ess[k] = 1 + ((r >> i) % 3); });
      const yieldPool = CAT.filter(x=>x.id!==c.id);
      const y1 = yieldPool[r % yieldPool.length], y2 = yieldPool[(r >> 3) % yieldPool.length];
      prof[c.id+'|'+s.id] = {
        cat: v.cats[r % v.cats.length],
        inherit: r % 3 === 0,
        mute: [],
        tags: v.tags.slice(0, tagCount),
        ess,
        on: s.mode !== 'none' && r % 7 !== 0,
        dc: s.dc ? String(s.dc) : '',
        progDc: 8 + (r % 12),
        groups: [{label:'Recovered', results:[{id:y1.id,name:y1.name,qty:1+(r%2)}]},{label:'Salvage', results:[{id:y2.id,name:y2.name,qty:1}]}],
        results: [{id:y1.id,name:y1.name,qty:1+(r%2)},{id:y2.id,name:y2.name,qty:1}],
        routing: {'Critical success':'Recovered','Success':'Recovered','Failure':'Salvage'},
        from: r % 11 === 0 ? {sys:'Mythwright Forge', date:'3 Aug'} : null
      };
    }));
    ESS0.forEach(e => SYS.forEach(s => { if (!VOCAB[s.v].ess.includes(e.id)) return; const r = hash(e.id+s.id+'e');

      essProf[e.id+'|'+s.id] = {on: a !== 0, inh:{link:a>3, macro:b>3},
        link: a < 6 ? EFFECT_ITEMS[b % EFFECT_ITEMS.length] : null,

        from: a === 7 ? {sys:'Mythwright Forge', date:'3 Aug'} : null}; }));

      twd:Object.fromEntries(TOOLS0.map((t,i)=>{ const r=hash(t.id+'tool');
        const mode = i%3===0 ? 'chance' : i%3===1 ? 'uses' : 'dice';
        return [t.id,{on:true, label:'', mode, chance:4+(r%9), uses:mode==='uses'?(1+r%4):1, formula:'1d20', below:1,
          immune:i===8, onBreak:i%4===0?'destroy':i%4===1?'broken':'replace', repl:i%4===2?CAT[(r%CAT.length)]. id:'',
          repair: i%4===1 ? [
            {id:'rp'+i+'a', kind:'comp', ref:CAT[r%CAT.length].id, tags:[], pol:'any', qty:2, alts:[]},
            {id:'rp'+i+'b', kind:'tag', ref:'', tags:[WTAGS0[r%WTAGS0.length]], pol:'any', qty:1, alts:[]}
          ] : [],
          prereqOn:i%3===0, prereqs:i%3===0?{[PREREQ[r%PREREQ.length].id]:true}:{}, prereqFail:'withhold',
          bonusOn:i%2===0, bonus:i%2===0?'prof':'', linked:t.id!=='pipe'}]; })),
      wBreakMode:'tool', sysBreak:{}, toolProf:Object.fromEntries(SYS.flatMap(sy=>TOOLS0.filter((t,i)=>hash(t.id+sy.id)%10>3).map((t,i)=>{
        const r=hash(t.id+sy.id+'p'), over=r%5===0;
        return [t.id+'|'+sy.id,{on:r%9!==0, inh:{brk:!over, ob:true, req:r%7!==0},
          brk:{mode:'chance', chance:5+(r%10), uses:2, formula:'1d20', below:1, immune:false},
          ob:{onBreak:'broken', repl:'', repair:[]},
          req:{prereqOn:r%7===0, prereqs:r%7===0?{[PREREQ[r%PREREQ.length].id]:true}:{}, prereqFail:'unusable', bonusOn:false, bonus:''}}]; }))),
      toolQ:'', toolInsp:'pick', toolId:null, toolTab:'ov', toolSort:'name', toolDir:'asc',
      stQ:'', stFilter:'in', stInsp:'pick', toolProfId:null, tpTab:'brk', stSort:'name', stDir:'asc',
      enTab:'main', prTab:'main', eeTab:'main', epTab:'main',
      pvActor:'', pvPre:{}, pvBroken:false, pvOpen:false, pvQ:'', rpOpen:'', rpQ:'', rpDrag:'',
      sopt:{}, svInput:{rcat:'',cat:'',tag:''}, svQ:{rcat:'',cat:'',tag:''}, svShow:{rcat:'all',cat:'all',tag:'all'},
      wvQ:{rcat:'',cat:'',tag:''}, wvSort:{rcat:'name',cat:'name',tag:'name'}, wvDir:{rcat:'asc',cat:'asc',tag:'asc'},
      navOpen:{rr:true, realms:true, craft:true, gath:true},
      esSort:'name', esSortDir:'asc', sleSort:'name', sleSortDir:'asc',
      insSysQ:'', insSysPage:0, esiSysQ:'', esiSysPage:0, tinsSysQ:'', tinsSysPage:0,
      wcls, wcats:WCATS0.slice(), wtags:WTAGS0.slice(), wcatInput:'', wtagInput:'', sleQ:'', sleFilter:'in',
      slQ:'', slCat:'all', slEss:'all', slSort:'name', slDir:'asc', slGroup:true, slInsp:null, profId:null, psel:{}, esel:{},
      ebMode:'add', ebSys:{}, ebArm:false, essGone:{}, ebSysQ:'', ebSysPage:0,
      bulk:{cat:'',tagAdd:{},tagRem:{},ess:{},sal:'',progOn:false,progDc:12},
      sbCatQ:'', sbCatPage:0, sbTagQ:'', sbTagPage:0, sbEssQ:'', sbEssPage:0, sbArm:false,
      sesel:{}, sleInsp:null, seiSysQ:'', seiSysPage:0,
      sbeState:'', sbeLink:'', sbeMacro:'', sbeDropL:false, sbeDropM:false, sbeArm:false,
      ebulk:{color:'',state:'',icon:'',iconQ:'',pick:false},
      essSel:'metal', essQ:'', essEntryId:null, essRowQ:'', essFilter:'all', essProfId:null, essDraft:false, iconPick:false, iconQ:'',
      modal:null, modalSel:{}, copyFrom:'', dirty:false, toast:null};
  }
  set(p){ this.setState(p); }
  flash(msg, sub, icon, color){ const t={msg,sub,icon:icon||'fa-solid fa-circle-check',color:color||'var(--success)'};
    this.setState({toast:t}); clearTimeout(this._t); this._t=setTimeout(()=>this.setState(s=>s.toast===t?{toast:null}:{}),5000); }
  sys(id){ return SYS.find(s=>s.id===(id||this.state.sysId)); }
  cat(id){ return CAT.find(c=>c.id===id); }
  pk(c,s){ return c+'|'+s; }
  has(c,s){ return !!this.state.prof[this.pk(c,s)]; }
  memberSys(cid){ return SYS.filter(s=>this.has(cid,s.id)); }
  essCompCount(eid,sid){ const S=this.state;
    return Object.keys(S.prof).filter(k=>{ const p=S.prof[k]; if(!p||!p.ess||p.ess[eid]===undefined) return false;
      return sid ? k.split('|')[1]===sid : true; }).length; }
  essRecipeCount(eid,sid){ const list = sid ? [this.sys(sid)] : SYS;
    return list.reduce((n,s)=>{ if(!this.hasEss(eid,s.id)) return n;
      let h=0; const k=eid+':'+s.id+'r'; for(let i=0;i<k.length;i++) h=(h*33+k.charCodeAt(i))&0xffff;
      return n + h%7; }, 0); }
  essEnabledCount(eid){ return SYS.filter(s=>{ const p=this.state.essProf[eid+'|'+s.id]; return p&&p.on; }).length; }
  sysPage(list, q, pg, qKey, pgKey){
    const per = 5, f = list.filter(x=>!q||x.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(f.length/per)), p = Math.min(pg, pages-1);
    const btn = on => 'width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:8.5px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);color:'+(on?'var(--text2)':'var(--disabled)')+';cursor:'+(on?'pointer':'not-allowed');
    return {rows:f.slice(p*per,(p+1)*per), empty:f.length===0,
      pageLabel:'Page '+(p+1)+' of '+pages,
      range: f.length ? 'Showing '+(p*per+1)+'\u2013'+Math.min(f.length,(p+1)*per)+' of '+f.length : 'Showing 0 of 0',
      prevStyle:btn(p>0), nextStyle:btn(p<pages-1),
      onQ:e=>this.set({[qKey]:e.target.value, [pgKey]:0}),
      onPrev:()=>{ if(p>0) this.set({[pgKey]:p-1}); }, onNext:()=>{ if(p<pages-1) this.set({[pgKey]:p+1}); }};
  }
  wclsOf(cid){ return this.state.wcls[cid] || {cat:'',tags:[]}; }
  svOwn(sid,kind){ return (this.state.sown[sid||this.state.sysId]||{})[kind] || []; }
  svWorld(kind){ const S=this.state; return kind==='rcat'?S.wrcats:kind==='cat'?S.wcats:S.wtags; }
  svOut(sid,kind,name){ return !!this.state.sopt[(sid||this.state.sysId)+'|'+kind+'|'+name]; }
  svIn(sid,kind){ return this.svWorld(kind).filter(x=>!this.svOut(sid,kind,x)); }
  catOpts(sid){ const own = this.svOwn(sid,'cat'), world = this.svIn(sid,'cat');
    return [...world.map(c=>({val:c, label:c+' \u00b7 world', world:true})),
            ...own.filter(c=>!world.includes(c)).map(c=>({val:c, label:c, world:false}))]; }
  effCat(cid,sid){ const p=this.state.prof[this.pk(cid,sid)]; if(!p) return '';
    if (p.inherit) return this.wclsOf(cid).cat || p.cat; return p.cat; }
  catInherited(cid,sid){ const p=this.state.prof[this.pk(cid,sid)];
    return !!(p && p.inherit && this.wclsOf(cid).cat); }
  effTags(cid,sid){ const p=this.state.prof[this.pk(cid,sid)];
    const w=this.wclsOf(cid).tags, mute=(p&&p.mute)||[];
    return {world:w.map(t=>({name:t, muted:mute.includes(t)})), own:(p&&p.tags)||[],
      all:[...w.filter(t=>!mute.includes(t)), ...((p&&p.tags)||[]).filter(t=>!w.includes(t))]}; }
  essOf(id){ return this.state.ess.find(e=>e.id===id) || this.state.ess[0]; }
  essWOf(eid){ return this.state.essW[eid] || {link:null, macro:null}; }
  setEssW(eid,patch){ const essW={...this.state.essW}; essW[eid]={...this.essWOf(eid),...patch}; this.setState({essW,dirty:true}); }
  effEss(eid,sid){ const w=this.essWOf(eid), p=sid?this.state.essProf[eid+'|'+sid]:null;
    if(!sid) return {on:true, link:w.link, macro:w.macro, inhLink:true, inhMacro:true, member:true};
    if(!p) return {on:false, link:w.link, macro:w.macro, inhLink:true, inhMacro:true, member:false};
    const inh=p.inh||{link:true,macro:true};
    return {on:!!p.on, link:inh.link?w.link:p.link, macro:inh.macro?w.macro:p.macro,
      inhLink:!!inh.link, inhMacro:!!inh.macro, member:true}; }
  essInhCount(eid,key){ return SYS.filter(sy=>{ const p=this.state.essProf[eid+'|'+sy.id]; return p && (p.inh||{})[key]!==false; }).length; }
  hasEss(eid,sid){ return !!this.state.essProf[eid+'|'+sid]; }
  essSysList(eid){ return SYS.filter(s=>this.hasEss(eid,s.id)); }
  sysEss(sid){ return this.state.ess.filter(e=>this.hasEss(e.id,sid)); }
  tool(tid){ return this.state.tools.find(t=>t.id===tid) || this.state.tools[0]; }
  twdOf(tid){ return this.state.twd[tid]; }
  tpOf(tid,sid){ return this.state.toolProf[tid+'|'+(sid||this.state.sysId)]; }
  hasTool(tid,sid){ return !!this.tpOf(tid,sid); }
  toolSysList(tid){ return SYS.filter(s=>this.hasTool(tid,s.id)); }
  sysToolList(sid){ return this.state.tools.filter(t=>this.hasTool(t.id,sid)); }
  breakModeOf(sid){ return sid ? (this.state.sysBreak[sid] || this.state.wBreakMode) : this.state.wBreakMode; }
  effTool(tid,sid){ const w=this.twdOf(tid), p=sid?this.tpOf(tid,sid):null;
    const brk = (p && !p.inh.brk) ? {...w, ...p.brk} : w;
    const ob  = (p && !p.inh.ob)  ? {...w, ...p.ob}  : w;
    const req = p ? {...w, ...p.req} : w;
    return {w, p, brk, ob, req, mode:this.breakModeOf(sid),
      inhBrk:!p || p.inh.brk, inhOb:!p || p.inh.ob, inhReq:!p,
      on: p ? p.on : w.on}; }
  brkLabel(tid,sid){ const e=this.effTool(tid,sid);
    if (e.mode==='check') return e.brk.immune ? 'Immune' : 'Roll to break';
    if (e.brk.mode==='uses') return (e.brk.uses===null||e.brk.uses<1||e.brk.uses>=99) ? 'Unlimited uses' : e.brk.uses+' use'+(e.brk.uses===1?'':'s');
    if (e.brk.mode==='dice') return e.brk.formula+' roll';
    return e.brk.chance<=0 ? 'Unbreakable' : e.brk.chance+'% break'; }
  brkNote(tid,sid){ const e=this.effTool(tid,sid);
    return e.mode==='check' ? 'Check-driven \u00b7 follows the crafting roll' : 'Tool-specific \u00b7 tracked per copy'; }
  obLabel(tid,sid){ const o=this.effTool(tid,sid).ob;
    return o.onBreak==='destroy' ? 'On break: destroy the item' : o.onBreak==='broken' ? 'On break: mark as broken' : 'On break: replace with component'; }
  repairRows(tid,sid){ const e=this.effTool(tid,sid); return (e.ob.repair||[]); }
  repairLabel(tid,sid){ const n=this.repairRows(tid,sid).length;
    return n ? 'Repair: '+n+' requirement'+(n===1?'':'s') : 'No repair route'; }
  reqName(m){ if(m.kind==='comp'){ const c=CAT.find(x=>x.id===m.ref); return c?c.name:'a component'; }
    if(m.kind==='ess'){ const e=this.state.ess.find(x=>x.id===m.ref); return e?e.name+' essence':'an essence'; }
    if(m.kind==='cur'){ const c=CURR.find(x=>x.val===m.ref); return c?m.qty+' '+m.ref:'currency'; }
    const t=(m.tags||[]); return t.length ? (m.pol==='all'?'all of ':'any of ')+t.join(', ') : 'a tagged item'; }
  repairSummary(tid,sid){ const rows=this.repairRows(tid,sid); if(!rows.length) return 'Nothing listed \u2014 a broken copy cannot be mended.';
    const one = m => (m.kind==='cur' ? this.reqName(m) : (m.qty>1?m.qty+'\u00d7 ':'')+this.reqName(m));
    return 'Mending consumes '+rows.map(r=>[r,...(r.alts||[])].map(one).join(' or ')).join(' + ')+'.'; }
  prereqLabel(tid,sid){ const r=this.effTool(tid,sid).req, n=Object.keys(r.prereqs||{}).filter(k=>r.prereqs[k]).length;
    return (!r.prereqOn||!n) ? 'No prerequisites to use' : n+' prerequisite'+(n===1?'':'s'); }
  bonusLabel(tid,sid){ const r=this.effTool(tid,sid).req; if(!r.bonusOn||!r.bonus) return 'No check bonus';
    const m=MODS.find(x=>x.expr===r.bonus); return 'Adds '+(m?m.label:(/^[0-9]/.test(r.bonus)?'':'@')+r.bonus); }
  modValue(actor,expr){ if(!actor||!expr) return null;
    if(/^[0-9]+$/.test(expr)) return Number(expr);
    if(/^\d*d\d+$/i.test(expr)) return null;
    const map={'prof':actor.prof,'prof * 2':actor.prof*2,'abilities.str.mod':actor.str,'abilities.int.mod':actor.int,
      'abilities.dex.mod':actor.dex,'tools.smith.total':actor.prof+(actor.pre&&actor.pre.smith?actor.str||0:0)};
    return map[expr]===undefined ? null : map[expr]; }
  toolIssues(tid,sid){ const e=this.effTool(tid,sid), w=e.w, out={pass:[],warn:[],block:[]};
    const add=(kind,group,label,note)=>out[kind].push({group,label,note});
    const scoped = (sid && e.p) ? true : false;
    /* a failure inside a section this system inherits is the world's to fix — warn here, never block */
    const dg=(kind,inherited,note)=>(kind==='block'&&sid&&inherited)
      ? ['warn', (note?note+' ':'')+'Inherited from the world Tool — fix it there.']
      : [kind, note];
    const addS=(kind,inherited,group,label,note)=>{ const [k,n]=dg(kind,inherited,note); add(k,group,label,n); };
    if (w.linked) add('pass','Linked item','A game-world Item is linked');
    else if (sid) add('warn','Linked item','World Tool has no linked Item','Fix it in the world Tools catalogue \u2014 read-only here.');
    else add('block','Linked item','No game-world Item is linked','Drop an Item on the Overview tab.');
    if (e.mode==='check') {
      if (e.brk.immune) add('pass','Breakage','Immune to check-driven breakage');
      else add('pass','Breakage','Breakable by the active check');
    } else if (e.brk.mode==='dice') {
      if ((e.brk.formula||'').trim()) add('pass','Breakage','Breakage roll has an expression');
      else addS('block',e.inhBrk,'Breakage','Breakage roll has no expression','A dice expression is required.');
    } else if (e.brk.mode==='uses') {
      if (e.brk.uses===null||e.brk.uses<1) add('pass','Breakage','Unlimited uses \u2014 never spent by use');
      else add('pass','Breakage','Uses per copy is set');
    } else if (e.brk.chance<=0) add('pass','Breakage','Unbreakable \u2014 0% chance per use');
    else add('pass','Breakage','Break chance is set');
    if (e.ob.onBreak==='replace') {
      if (e.ob.repl) add('pass','Breakage','Replacement component is set');
      else addS('block',e.inhOb,'Breakage','No replacement component chosen','Replace with component needs a managed Component.');
    } else if (e.ob.onBreak==='broken') {
      const rr=e.ob.repair||[];
      if (!rr.length) add('warn','Breakage','No repair materials listed','Marked-broken Tools with no repair route can never be mended.');
      else { const bad=rr.concat(...rr.map(r=>r.alts||[])).filter(m=>m.kind==='tag'?!(m.tags||[]).length:!m.ref);
        if (bad.length) addS('block',e.inhOb,'Breakage',bad.length+' repair requirement'+(bad.length===1?' is':'s are')+' empty','Pick a component, tag, essence or currency on every row.');
        else add('pass','Breakage',rr.length+' repair requirement'+(rr.length===1?'':'s')+' listed'); }
    }
    else add('pass','Breakage','On-break action is set');
    const pn = Object.keys(e.req.prereqs||{}).filter(k=>e.req.prereqs[k]).length;
    if (!e.req.prereqOn) add('pass','Requirements','Character prerequisites are off');
    else if (pn) add('pass','Requirements','Character prerequisites are complete');
    else add('warn','Requirements','Prerequisites are on with nothing selected','Every character passes, so the gate does nothing.');
    if (!e.req.bonusOn) add('pass','Requirements','No check bonus to validate');
    else if ((e.req.bonus||'').trim()) add('pass','Requirements','Bonus expression is set');
    else addS('block',e.inhReq,'Requirements','Bonus is on with no expression');
    if (scoped) add('pass','System rules','Rules resolve in this system',
      [e.inhBrk?'breakage inherited':'breakage overridden', e.inhOb?'on break inherited':'on break overridden', 'requirements are this system\u2019s own'].join(' \u00b7 '));
    return out; }
  compIssues(cid,sid){ const out={pass:[],warn:[],block:[]}, add=(k,g,l,n)=>out[k].push({group:g,label:l,note:n});
    const c=this.cat(cid), w=this.wclsOf(cid), broken=cid==='grif', unlinked=!!this.state.unlinked[cid];
    if (broken) add('block','Source','Linked source item is missing','Recipes using it fail closed until the link is restored.');
    else if (unlinked) add('block','Source','No source item linked','Every component needs one source item.');
    else add('pass','Source','A source item is linked');
    add(c.name?'pass':'block','Identity', c.name?'Name is set':'Name is empty');
    if (w.cat) add('pass','World classification','World category is set');
    else add('warn','World classification','No world category','Systems that inherit fall back to their own list.');
    if (!w.tags.length) add('warn','World classification','No world tags','Nothing merges into system tag lists.');
    else add('pass','World classification',w.tags.length+' world tag'+(w.tags.length===1?'':'s')+' set');
    if (!sid) return out;
    const p=this.state.prof[this.pk(cid,sid)], sy=this.sys(sid);
    if (!p) { add('block','System rules','No rules in '+sy.name); return out; }
    if (this.effCat(cid,sid)) add('pass','System rules','Category resolves in '+sy.name);
    else add('block','System rules','No category resolves here','Inheriting from a world value that is not set.');
    const ek=Object.keys(p.ess||{});
    if (ek.some(k=>!this.hasEss(k,sid))) add('block','System rules','Carries an essence with no rules in this system');
    else if (ek.length) add('pass','System rules',ek.length+' essence value'+(ek.length===1?'':'s')+' resolve');
    else add('warn','System rules','No essence values','Recipes here cannot match it by essence.');
    if (sy.mode==='none') add('pass','Salvage','Salvage is off system-wide');
    else if (!p.on) add('pass','Salvage','Salvage is off for this component');
    else if (sy.mode==='progressive') { if (p.progDc) add('pass','Salvage','Progressive DC is set'); else add('block','Salvage','Progressive salvage with no DC');
      if (!p.results.length) add('block','Salvage','Salvage is on with no results'); else add('pass','Salvage',p.results.length+' salvage result'+(p.results.length===1?'':'s')); }
    else { const n=p.groups.reduce((a,g)=>a+g.results.length,0);
      if (!n) add('block','Salvage','Salvage is on with no results'); else add('pass','Salvage',n+' salvage result'+(n===1?'':'s'));
      if (!p.dc && sy.dc) add('warn','Salvage','No DC on this component','Falls back to the system DC of '+sy.dc+'.'); }
    if (p.results && p.results.some(r=>!this.has(r.id,sid))) add('block','Salvage','A yield has no rules in this system');
    return out; }
  essIssues(eid,sid){ const out={pass:[],warn:[],block:[]}, add=(k,g,l,n)=>out[k].push({group:g,label:l,note:n});
    const e=this.essOf(eid), wd=this.essWOf(eid);
    if (!sid) { if (wd.link) add('pass','World defaults','Default effect source is set',wd.link.name+' \u2014 '+this.essInhCount(eid,'link')+' systems inherit it');
      else add('warn','World defaults','No default effect source','Systems that inherit gain no active effects on craft.');
      if (wd.macro) add('pass','World defaults','Default macro is set',wd.macro.name+' \u2014 '+this.essInhCount(eid,'macro')+' systems inherit it');
      else add('warn','World defaults','No default macro','Nothing runs on craft for systems that inherit.'); }
    add(e.name?'pass':'block','Identity', e.name?'Name is set':'Name is empty');
    add(e.icon?'pass':'block','Identity', e.icon?'Icon is set':'No icon chosen');
    if (e.desc) add('pass','Identity','Description is set'); else add('warn','Identity','No description','GMs pick essences by description in the recipe editor.');
    const used=this.essSysList(eid).length;
    if (used) add('pass','Identity','Used by '+used+' system'+(used===1?'':'s'));
    else add('warn','Identity','No system has rules for it','Nothing reads its values yet.');
    if (!sid) return out;
    const p=this.state.essProf[eid+'|'+sid], sy=this.sys(sid);
    if (!p) { add('block','System rules','No rules in '+sy.name); return out; }
    if (!p.on) { add('pass','System rules','Disabled here \u2014 nothing runs on craft'); return out; }
    add('pass','System rules','Enabled in '+sy.name);
    const ef=this.effEss(eid,sid);
    if (ef.link) add('pass','On craft','Effect source resolves', ef.inhLink?'Inherited from the world default.':'Overridden for this system.');
    else add('warn','On craft','No effect source','Crafted items gain no active effects from it here.');
    if (ef.macro) add('pass','On craft','Macro is set', ef.inhMacro?'Inherited from the world default.':'Overridden for this system.');
    else add('warn','On craft','No macro','Nothing runs against the item data.');
    const carriers=this.essCompCount(eid,sid);
    if (carriers) add('pass','On craft',carriers+' component'+(carriers===1?'':'s')+' carry it here');
    else add('block','On craft','Enabled but no component carries it','Recipes requiring it can never be satisfied.');
    return out; }
  setEssProf(eid,sid,patch){ const essProf={...this.state.essProf}; essProf[eid+'|'+sid]={...essProf[eid+'|'+sid],...patch}; this.setState({essProf,dirty:true}); }
  pageOf(list, pg, key, per){ per = per || 5;
    const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(list.length/per)), p = Math.min(pg||0, pages-1);
    const btn = on => 'width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:8.5px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);color:'+(on?'var(--text2)':'var(--disabled)')+';cursor:'+(on?'pointer':'not-allowed');
    return {rows:list.slice(p*per,(p+1)*per), empty:list.length===0,
      pageLabel:'Page '+(p+1)+' of '+pages,
      range: list.length ? 'Showing '+(p*per+1)+'\u2013'+Math.min(list.length,(p+1)*per)+' of '+list.length : 'Showing 0 of 0',
      prevStyle:btn(p>0), nextStyle:btn(p<pages-1),
      onPrev:()=>{ if(p>0) this.set({[key]:p-1}); }, onNext:()=>{ if(p<pages-1) this.set({[key]:p+1}); }};
  }
  removeSelFromSys(){
    const S = this.state, ids = Object.keys(S.psel), sid = S.sysId, sys = this.sys();
    if (!ids.length) return;
    if (!S.sbArm) { this.set({sbArm:true}); return; }
    const prof = {...S.prof}; let n = 0;
    ids.forEach(cid=>{ const k=this.pk(cid,sid); if (prof[k]) { delete prof[k]; n++; } });
    this.setState({prof, psel:{}, sbArm:false, dirty:true});
    this.flash(n+' rule set'+(n===1?'':'s')+' removed from '+sys.name,'The catalogue entries and every other system are untouched.','fa-solid fa-arrow-right-from-bracket','var(--warning)');
  }
  applySysEssBulk(){
    const S = this.state, ids = Object.keys(S.sesel), sid = S.sysId, sys = this.sys();
    if (!ids.length) return;
    if (!S.sbeState && !S.sbeLink && !S.sbeMacro) { this.flash('Nothing staged','Stage an enabled state, an effect source or a macro first.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    const essProf = {...S.essProf}, bits = []; let n = 0;
    ids.forEach(eid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+sid, p=essProf[k]; if(!p) return; const x={...p};
      if (S.sbeState) x.on = S.sbeState==='on';
      if (S.sbeLink) x.link = S.sbeLink==='clear' ? null : S.sbeLink;
      if (S.sbeMacro) x.macro = S.sbeMacro==='clear' ? null : S.sbeMacro;
      essProf[k]=x; n++; });
    if (!n) { this.flash('Nothing to change','None of the selected essences has rules in '+sys.name+' yet. Add them first.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    if (S.sbeState) bits.push(S.sbeState==='on'?'enabled':'disabled');
    if (S.sbeLink) bits.push(S.sbeLink==='clear'?'effect source unlinked':'effects from '+S.sbeLink.name);
    if (S.sbeMacro) bits.push(S.sbeMacro==='clear'?'macro removed':'macro '+S.sbeMacro.name);
    this.setState({essProf, sesel:{}, sbeState:'', sbeLink:'', sbeMacro:'', sbeDropL:false, sbeDropM:false, sbeArm:false, dirty:true});
    this.flash(n+' essence rule set'+(n===1?'':'s')+' updated in '+sys.name, bits.join(' \u00b7 '),'fa-solid fa-check-double');
  }
  addSelEssToSys(){
    const S = this.state, ids = Object.keys(S.sesel), sid = S.sysId, sys = this.sys();
    if (!ids.length) return;
    const essProf = {...S.essProf}; let n = 0;
    ids.forEach(eid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+sid; if(!essProf[k]){ essProf[k]={on:true,inh:{link:true,macro:true},link:null,macro:null,from:null}; n++; } });
    if (!n) { this.flash('Nothing to add','Every selected essence already has rules in '+sys.name+'.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    this.setState({essProf, dirty:true});
    this.flash(n+' rule set'+(n===1?'':'s')+' created in '+sys.name,'They start enabled, with no effect source and no macro.','fa-solid fa-plus');
  }
  removeSelEssFromSys(){
    const S = this.state, ids = Object.keys(S.sesel), sid = S.sysId, sys = this.sys();
    if (!ids.length) return;
    if (!S.sbeArm) { this.set({sbeArm:true}); return; }
    const essProf = {...S.essProf}; let n = 0;
    ids.forEach(eid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+sid; if (essProf[k]) { delete essProf[k]; n++; } });
    this.setState({essProf, sesel:{}, sbeArm:false, dirty:true});
    this.flash(n+' essence'+(n===1?'':'s')+' removed from '+sys.name,'The world definitions and every other system are untouched.','fa-solid fa-arrow-right-from-bracket','var(--warning)');
  }
  applyBulk(){
    const S = this.state, B = S.bulk, ids = Object.keys(S.psel), sid = S.sysId, sys = this.sys();
    const tagAdd = Object.keys(B.tagAdd), tagRem = Object.keys(B.tagRem), essKeys = Object.keys(B.ess);
    if (!B.cat && !tagAdd.length && !tagRem.length && !essKeys.length && !B.sal && !B.progOn) {
      this.flash('Nothing to apply','Set a category, tags, essences or salvage first.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    const prof = {...S.prof};
    ids.forEach(cid => { const k = this.pk(cid,sid), p = prof[k]; if (!p) return; const x = {...p};
      if (B.cat === '__inherit') x.inherit = true;
      else if (B.cat) { x.cat = B.cat; x.inherit = false; }
      if (tagAdd.length || tagRem.length) x.tags = [...new Set([...x.tags.filter(t=>!B.tagRem[t]), ...tagAdd])];
      if (essKeys.length) { const es = {...x.ess}; essKeys.forEach(e=>{ if (B.ess[e] > 0) es[e] = B.ess[e]; else delete es[e]; }); x.ess = es; }
      if (B.sal) x.on = B.sal === 'on';
      if (B.progOn && sys.mode === 'progressive') x.progDc = B.progDc;
      prof[k] = x; });
    const bits = [];
    if (B.cat) bits.push(B.cat==='__inherit'?'category inherited':'category');
    if (tagAdd.length) bits.push(tagAdd.length+' tag'+(tagAdd.length===1?'':'s')+' added');
    if (tagRem.length) bits.push(tagRem.length+' removed');
    if (essKeys.length) bits.push('essences');
    if (B.sal) bits.push('salvage '+B.sal);
    if (B.progOn && sys.mode==='progressive') bits.push('DC '+B.progDc);
    this.setState({prof, psel:{}, bulk:{cat:'',tagAdd:{},tagRem:{},ess:{},sal:'',progOn:false,progDc:12}, sbCatQ:'', sbCatPage:0, sbTagQ:'', sbTagPage:0, sbEssQ:'', sbEssPage:0, sbArm:false, dirty:true});
    this.flash(ids.length+' components updated in '+sys.name, bits.join(' · '),'fa-solid fa-check-double');
  }
  commitEssBulk(){
    const S = this.state, ids = Object.keys(S.esel);
    if (!ids.length) return;
    const staged = Object.keys(S.ebSys).filter(sid=>SYS.some(x=>x.id===sid));
    const EB = S.ebulk, adding = S.ebMode!=='remove';
    const hasFields = !!(EB.icon || EB.color || EB.state);
    if (!staged.length && !hasFields) { this.flash('Nothing staged','Tick the systems this change applies to, or stage an icon, colour or state.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    let ess = S.ess, essProf = {...S.essProf}, bits = [], moved = 0;
    if (EB.icon || EB.color) { ess = S.ess.map(e=>{ if(!ids.includes(e.id)) return e; const x={...e};
        if (EB.icon) x.icon = EB.icon; if (EB.color) x.color = EB.color; return x; });
      if (EB.icon) bits.push('icon'); if (EB.color) bits.push('colour'); }
    if (staged.length) { ids.forEach(eid=>staged.forEach(sid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+sid;
        if (adding) { if(!essProf[k]){ essProf[k]={on:true,inh:{link:true,macro:true},link:null,macro:null,from:null}; moved++; } }
        else if (essProf[k]) { delete essProf[k]; moved++; } }));
      bits.push(moved+(adding?' rule sets created':' rule sets removed')+' in '+staged.length+' system'+(staged.length===1?'':'s')); }
    if (EB.state) { let n=0; ids.forEach(eid=>Object.keys(essProf).forEach(k=>{ if(k.split('|')[0]===eid){ essProf[k]={...essProf[k], on:EB.state==='on'}; n++; } }));
      bits.push(n+(EB.state==='on'?' enabled':' disabled')); }
    if (!bits.length) { this.flash('Nothing to change','Those systems already match the change you picked.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    this.setState({ess, essProf, esel:{}, ebSys:{}, ebArm:false, ebulk:{color:'',state:'',icon:'',iconQ:'',pick:false}, dirty:true});
    this.flash(ids.length+(ids.length===1?' essence committed':' essences committed'), bits.join(' \u00b7 '),'fa-solid fa-check-double');
  }
  applyEssBulk(patch){
    const S = this.state, ids = Object.keys(S.esel);
    if (!ids.length) return;
    if (patch.remove) { const essProf = {...S.essProf}; let n=0;
      ids.forEach(eid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+S.sysId; if (essProf[k]) { delete essProf[k]; n++; } });
      this.setState({essProf, esel:{}, dirty:true});
      this.flash(n+' removed from '+this.sys().name,'Component rules there lose those values.','fa-solid fa-arrow-right-from-bracket','var(--warning)'); return; }
    const EB = S.ebulk, bits = [];
    if (!EB.icon && !EB.color && !EB.state) { this.flash('Nothing staged','Pick an icon, a colour or an enabled state first.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
    let ess = S.ess, essProf = {...S.essProf};
    if (EB.icon || EB.color) { ess = S.ess.map(e=>{ if (!ids.includes(e.id)) return e; const x={...e};
        if (EB.icon) x.icon = EB.icon; if (EB.color) x.color = EB.color; return x; });
      if (EB.icon) bits.push('icon'); if (EB.color) bits.push('colour'); }
    if (EB.state) { let n=0; ids.forEach(eid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+S.sysId; if (essProf[k]) { essProf[k]={...essProf[k], on:EB.state==='on'}; n++; } });
      bits.push(n+(EB.state==='on'?' enabled':' disabled')+' in '+this.sys().name); }
    this.setState({ess, essProf, ebulk:{color:'',state:'',icon:'',iconQ:'',pick:false}, dirty:true});
    this.flash(ids.length+' essences updated', bits.join(' · '),'fa-solid fa-check-double');
  }
  essSummary(eid,sid){
    const p = this.state.essProf[eid+'|'+sid]; if (!p) return 'No rules — this system does not use it';
    if (!p.on) return 'Disabled in this system';
    const e = this.effEss(eid,sid), bits = [];
    bits.push((e.link ? 'Effects from ' + e.link.name : 'No effect source') + (e.inhLink?'':' (override)'));
    bits.push((e.macro ? 'Macro: ' + e.macro.name : 'no macro') + (e.inhMacro?'':' (override)'));
    return bits.join(' · ');
  }
  setProf(cid,sid,patch){ const prof={...this.state.prof}; prof[this.pk(cid,sid)]={...prof[this.pk(cid,sid)],...patch}; this.setState({prof,dirty:true}); }
  addProf(cid,sid,fromSid){
    const prof={...this.state.prof}, s=this.sys(sid), r=hash(cid+sid);
    const v={...VOCAB[s.v], cats:this.svOwn(sid,'cat'), tags:this.svOwn(sid,'tag')};
    const src = fromSid ? prof[this.pk(cid,fromSid)] : null;
    const base = {cat:v.cats[0], inherit:true, mute:[], tags:[], ess:{}, on:s.mode!=='none', dc:s.dc?String(s.dc):'', progDc:10,
      groups:[{label:'Recovered',results:[]},{label:'Salvage',results:[]}], results:[], routing:{'Critical success':'Recovered','Success':'Recovered','Failure':'Salvage'}, from:null};
    prof[this.pk(cid,sid)] = src ? {...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(src)),
      cat: v.cats.includes(src.cat)?src.cat:v.cats[0],
      tags: src.tags.filter(t=>v.tags.includes(t)),
      ess: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(src.ess).filter(([k])=>v.ess.includes(k))),
      from:{sys:this.sys(fromSid).name, date:'today'}} : base;
    return prof;
  }
  recipeUse(cid,sid){
    if (!this.has(cid,sid)) return 0;
    let h = 0; const k = cid+':'+sid;
    for (let i=0;i<k.length;i++) h = (h*31 + k.charCodeAt(i)) & 0xffff;
    return h % 5;               /* 0-4 recipes reference it in that system */
  }
  recipeTotal(cid){ return SYS.reduce((n,s)=>n+this.recipeUse(cid,s.id), 0); }
  toolRecipeUse(tid,sid){ if(!this.hasTool(tid,sid)) return 0;
    let h=0; const k=tid+':'+sid+':tr'; for(let i=0;i<k.length;i++) h=(h*31+k.charCodeAt(i))&0xffff;
    return h % 6; }
  summary(cid,sid){
    const p = this.state.prof[this.pk(cid,sid)]; if (!p) return 'No rules — invisible to recipes in this system';
    const s = this.sys(sid), bits = [p.cat, p.tags.length+' tag'+(p.tags.length===1?'':'s')];
    const ek = Object.keys(p.ess); if (ek.length) bits.push(ek.map(k=>this.essOf(k).name+' '+p.ess[k]).join(', '));
    if (s.mode==='none') bits.push('no salvage in this system');
    else if (!p.on) bits.push('salvage off');
    else if (s.mode==='progressive') bits.push('progressive, DC '+p.progDc+', '+p.results.length+' results');
    else bits.push(MODE[s.mode].label.toLowerCase()+(p.dc?', DC '+p.dc:'')+', '+p.groups.reduce((a,g)=>a+g.results.length,0)+' results');
    return bits.join(' · ');
  }

  renderVals(){
    const S = this.state, sys = this.sys(), d = {sysCount:SYS.length, sysName:sys.name, sysId:S.sysId, dirty:S.dirty};
    const worldView = S.view==='catalogue'||S.view==='entry'||S.view==='essences'||S.view==='essEntry'||(S.view==='stub'&&S.stub&&S.stub.scope==='World');
    d.titlebar = worldView ? 'The Aetherlands' : 'Crafting Systems';
    d.scopeLabel = worldView ? 'World' : sys.name;
    d.scopeBadge = worldView ? 'padding:2px 8px;border-radius:5px;background:var(--info-soft);border:1px solid var(--info-border);font:700 9px var(--sans);letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--info-text)'
      : 'padding:2px 8px;border-radius:5px;background:var(--surface-raised);border:1px solid var(--border);font:700 9px var(--sans);letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text2)';
    const liveCat = CAT.filter(c=>!S.gone[c.id]);
    const liveEssCount = S.ess.filter(e=>!S.essGone[e.id]).length;
    d.statusNote = worldView ? liveCat.length+' components · '+liveEssCount+' essences · shared by '+SYS.length+' systems' : MODE[sys.mode].label+' salvage · '+this.sysEss(S.sysId).length+' essences · '+CAT.filter(c=>this.has(c.id,S.sysId)).length+' components with rules';
    d.onSys = e=>this.set({sysId:e.target.value, view:'system', slInsp:null});
    d.sysOpts = SYS.map(s=>({val:s.id,label:s.name}));
    d.onAllSystems = ()=>this.set({view:'catalogue'});
    d.onStub = ()=>this.flash('Prototype stub','Registering new items is outside this study.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)');

    const navRow = (i, activeK, kid) => { const on = i.k===activeK;
      return {label:i.label, icon:i.icon, meta:i.meta||'',
        style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;padding:'+(kid?'7px 9px':'8px 10px')+';border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font:'+(on?'600':'500')+' '+(kid?'11.5':'12')+'px var(--sans);'+(on?'background:var(--accent-soft);border:1px solid var(--accent-border);color:var(--accent)':'color:'+(i.dim?'var(--disabled)':'var(--muted)')+';border:1px solid transparent'),
        metaStyle:'margin-left:auto;font:'+(i.prem?'700 8px var(--sans);letter-spacing:.1em;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:5px;background:rgba(201,161,94,.12);border:1px solid rgba(201,161,94,.42)':i.soon?'600 8.5px var(--sans);padding:1px 6px;border-radius:5px;background:var(--surface-soft)':'700 9.5px var(--mono)')+';color:'+(i.prem?'#C9A15E':on?'var(--accent)':'var(--subtle)'),
        onClick:i.onClick, chevron:'', chevStyle:'display:none', showKids:false, kids:[]};
    };
    const mkNav = (items, activeK) => items.map(i => {
      if (!i.children) return navRow(i, activeK, false);
      const openG = !!this.state.navOpen[i.k];
      const kidActive = i.children.some(c=>c.k===activeK);
      const r = navRow({...i, k: kidActive&&!openG ? activeK : i.k+'__group'}, activeK, false);
      r.onClick = ()=>this.setState({navOpen:{...this.state.navOpen, [i.k]:!openG}});
      r.chevron = openG ? 'fa-solid fa-chevron-up' : 'fa-solid fa-chevron-down';
      r.chevStyle = 'flex:0 0 auto;margin-left:auto;font-size:8.5px;color:var(--subtle)';
      r.showKids = openG;
      r.kids = i.children.map(c=>navRow(c, activeK, true));
      return r;
    });
    const stub = (scope,label,icon,title,body,parent)=>()=>this.set({view:'stub', stub:{scope,label,icon,title,body,parent}});
    const activeWorld = S.view==='catalogue'||S.view==='entry' ? 'cat' : (S.view==='tools'||S.view==='toolEntry') ? 'wtools' : S.view==='wvocab' ? 'wvocab' : (S.view==='essences'||S.view==='essEntry') ? 'ess' : (S.view==='stub'&&S.stub&&S.stub.scope==='World'? S.stub.key : null);
    d.worldNav = mkNav([
      {k:'cat', label:'Component catalogue', icon:'fa-solid fa-cubes-stacked', meta:liveCat.length, onClick:()=>this.set({view:'catalogue'})},
      {k:'wvocab', label:'Tags & Categories', icon:'fa-solid fa-tags', meta:S.wrcats.length+S.wcats.length+S.wtags.length, onClick:()=>this.set({view:'wvocab'})},
      {k:'ess', label:'Essence Catalogue', icon:'fa-solid fa-flask-vial', meta:liveEssCount, onClick:()=>this.set({view:'essences'})},
      {k:'wtools', label:'Tools Catalogue', icon:'fa-solid fa-screwdriver-wrench', meta:S.tools.length, onClick:()=>this.set({view:'tools'})},
      {k:'parties', label:'Parties', icon:'fa-solid fa-users', meta:3, onClick:stub('World','Parties','fa-solid fa-users','Already world-scoped','Lifted in 1.26.0 alongside currency. Shown here only to place the catalogue in the same section of the rail.')},
      {k:'dt', label:'Downtime', icon:'fa-solid fa-hourglass-half', meta:'PREMIUM', prem:true, children:[
        {k:'dtTrack', label:'Tracking', icon:'fa-solid fa-chart-simple', onClick:stub('World','Downtime tracking','fa-solid fa-chart-simple','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Advance world time, credit days by hand and resolve finished work. It reads the shared parties, so every system sees the same characters.','Downtime')},
        {k:'dtActs', label:'Activities', icon:'fa-solid fa-list-check', meta:14, onClick:stub('World','Downtime activities','fa-solid fa-list-check','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Reusable activity definitions. Craft rewards name a recipe, which is why an activity can reach into any system\u2019s recipes and the shared catalogues.','Downtime')},
        {k:'dtFac', label:'Factions', icon:'fa-solid fa-flag', meta:6, onClick:stub('World','Factions','fa-solid fa-flag','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Standing gates which activities can run and which rewards unlock.','Downtime')},
        {k:'dtSet', label:'Settings', icon:'fa-solid fa-sliders', onClick:stub('World','Downtime settings','fa-solid fa-sliders','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Activity limits and time handling for every downtime activity in the world.','Downtime')}
      ]},
      {k:'econ', label:'Economy', icon:'fa-solid fa-chart-line', meta:'PREMIUM', prem:true, children:[
        {k:'econSet', label:'Settlements', icon:'fa-solid fa-city', meta:9, onClick:stub('World','Settlements','fa-solid fa-city','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Levels, stocks and gates per settlement. Automation advances or declines on a tick; manual mode surfaces readiness only.','Economy')},
        {k:'econBiz', label:'Businesses', icon:'fa-solid fa-shop', meta:23, onClick:stub('World','Businesses','fa-solid fa-shop','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Production lines attached to a settlement. Artisan-craft outputs name recipes, so they read the shared component catalogue.','Economy')},
        {k:'econTrade', label:'Trade routes', icon:'fa-solid fa-truck-fast', meta:11, onClick:stub('World','Trade routes','fa-solid fa-truck-fast','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Moves resources between settlements along the shared realms and map region links.','Economy')},
        {k:'econRes', label:'Resources', icon:'fa-solid fa-boxes-stacked', meta:16, onClick:stub('World','Resources','fa-solid fa-boxes-stacked','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','The resource categories settlements stock and businesses produce, and how each maps onto catalogue components.','Economy')},
        {k:'econCfg', label:'Settings', icon:'fa-solid fa-gear', onClick:stub('World','Economy settings','fa-solid fa-gear','Premium \u00b7 world-scoped','Tick length, automation defaults and upkeep behaviour for the whole world.','Economy')}
      ]},
      {k:'realms', label:'Realms & travel', icon:'fa-solid fa-route', children:[
        {k:'realmList', label:'Realms', icon:'fa-solid fa-mountain-sun', meta:7, onClick:stub('World','Realms','fa-solid fa-mountain-sun','Already world-scoped','Lifted in 1.27.0. One participation flag per system, no per-entry customisation — which is why it was the easy case.','Realms & travel')},
        {k:'mapLinks', label:'Map Region Links', icon:'fa-solid fa-map-location-dot', meta:12, onClick:stub('World','Map Region Links','fa-solid fa-map-location-dot','Already world-scoped','Binds scene regions to realms, so travel and gathering resolve against the same geography in every system.','Realms & travel')}
      ]},
      {k:'rr', label:'Rules & Resources', icon:'fa-solid fa-scale-balanced', children:[
        {k:'currency', label:'Currency', icon:'fa-solid fa-coins', meta:5, onClick:stub('World','Currency','fa-solid fa-coins','Already world-scoped','Lifted in 1.26.0.','Rules & Resources')},
        {k:'mods', label:'Modifiers', icon:'fa-solid fa-sliders', meta:12, onClick:stub('World','Modifiers','fa-solid fa-sliders','Already world-scoped','Lifted with the character libraries in 1.28.0.','Rules & Resources')},
        {k:'prereq', label:'Prerequisites', icon:'fa-solid fa-list-check', meta:9, onClick:stub('World','Prerequisites','fa-solid fa-list-check','Already world-scoped','Shared gates — skills, items, flags — that any system’s recipes and books can require.','Rules & Resources')}
      ]}
    ], activeWorld);
    const activeSys = S.view==='system'||S.view==='profile' ? 'comp' : (S.view==='sysTools'||S.view==='toolProfile') ? 'tools' : S.view==='svocab' ? 'vocab' : (S.view==='essProfile'||S.view==='sysEss') ? 'sess' : (S.view==='stub'&&S.stub&&S.stub.scope!=='World'? S.stub.key : null);
    d.sysNav = mkNav([
      {k:'ov', label:'System Overview', icon:'fa-solid fa-gauge', onClick:stub(sys.name,'System Overview','fa-solid fa-gauge','Unchanged by this lift','Counts and health for this system. It reads the per-system rules, so its component count becomes "components with rules in this system".')},
      {k:'craft', label:'Crafting', icon:'fa-solid fa-hammer', children:[
        {k:'rec', label:'Recipes', icon:'fa-solid fa-scroll', meta:18, onClick:stub(sys.name,'Recipes','fa-solid fa-scroll','Unchanged by this lift','Ingredient and result pickers list only the components this system has rules for — which is what keeps validation meaningful.','Crafting')},
        ...(sys.know ? [
          {k:'books', label:'Books & Scrolls', icon:'fa-solid fa-book', meta:6, onClick:stub(sys.name,'Books & Scrolls','fa-solid fa-book','Knowledge mode','Recipes are learned from in-world documents. Only knowledge-mode systems have these.','Crafting')},
          {k:'know', label:'Knowledge', icon:'fa-solid fa-brain', meta:14, onClick:stub(sys.name,'Knowledge','fa-solid fa-brain','Knowledge mode','What each character has learned, and from which book or scroll.','Crafting')}
        ] : [
          {k:'access', label:'Access', icon:'fa-solid fa-key', meta:11, onClick:stub(sys.name,'Access','fa-solid fa-key','Manual mode','You switch each recipe on or off per character yourself. Mutually exclusive with Knowledge and Books & Scrolls.','Crafting')}
        ]),
        {k:'craftSet', label:'Settings', icon:'fa-solid fa-gear', onClick:stub(sys.name,'Settings','fa-solid fa-gear','Unchanged by this lift','Salvage mode, essences and tags for crafting in this system. Switching salvage off here is what empties the salvage section of every component in this system.','Crafting')}
      ]},
      {k:'gath', label:'Gathering', icon:'fa-solid fa-seedling', meta:69, children:[
        {k:'envs', label:'Environments', icon:'fa-solid fa-tree', meta:18, onClick:stub(sys.name,'Environments','fa-solid fa-tree','Unchanged by this lift','Where gathering happens. Environments draw on the shared realms and map region links.','Gathering')},
        {k:'tasks', label:'Tasks', icon:'fa-solid fa-list-check', meta:22, onClick:stub(sys.name,'Tasks','fa-solid fa-list-check','Unchanged by this lift','What a character does to gather. Yields reference components this system has rules for.','Gathering')},

            {k:'vocab', label:'Tags & Categories', icon:'fa-solid fa-tags', meta:this.svIn(S.sysId,'rcat').length+this.svIn(S.sysId,'cat').length+this.svIn(S.sysId,'tag').length+this.svOwn(S.sysId,'rcat').length+this.svOwn(S.sysId,'cat').length+this.svOwn(S.sysId,'tag').length, onClick:()=>this.set({view:'svocab'})},

        {k:'chkCraft', label:'Crafting', icon:'fa-solid fa-hammer', onClick:stub(sys.name,'Crafting','fa-solid fa-hammer','Unchanged by this lift','The crafting check for this system. Difficulty tiers live here, which is why component rules store a DC and never a shared tier.','Checks')},
        {k:'chkSal', label:'Salvage', icon:'fa-solid fa-recycle', onClick:stub(sys.name,'Salvage','fa-solid fa-recycle','Unchanged by this lift','The salvage check. It reads each component\u2019s rules in this system, including the progressive DC.','Checks')},

      {k:'graph', label:'Graph', icon:'fa-solid fa-diagram-project', meta:'Soon', soon:true, dim:true, onClick:()=>{}}
    ], activeSys);

    d.vCat = S.view==='catalogue'; d.vEntry = S.view==='entry'; d.vSys = S.view==='system'; d.vProfile = S.view==='profile';
    const wcatUse = c => CAT.filter(x=>!S.gone[x.id] && this.wclsOf(x.id).cat===c).length;
    const wtagUse = t => CAT.filter(x=>!S.gone[x.id] && this.wclsOf(x.id).tags.includes(t)).length;
    const wrcatUse = c => { let h=0; for(let i=0;i<c.length;i++) h=(h*31+c.charCodeAt(i))&0xffff; return h%24; };
    const wAddStyle = on => 'flex:0 0 auto;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;height:32px;padding:0 13px;border-radius:7px;font:600 10.5px var(--sans);'+(on
      ? 'background:var(--accent);border:1px solid var(--accent-border);color:var(--on-accent);cursor:pointer'
      : 'background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--disabled);cursor:default');
    const addW = (key, listKey, label) => { const raw=(this.state[key]||'').trim(); if(!raw) return;
      if (this.state[listKey].includes(raw)) { this.set({[key]:''}); this.flash('Already in the list', raw+' is already a world '+label+'.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
      this.setState({[listKey]:[...this.state[listKey], raw], [key]:'', dirty:true});
      this.flash('World '+label+' added', raw+' is now available to every system.','fa-solid fa-plus'); };
    const wvPanel = cfg => {
      const q = (S.wvQ[cfg.k]||'').trim().toLowerCase();
      const dir = S.wvDir[cfg.k]==='desc' ? -1 : 1;
      let rows = this.state[cfg.listKey].filter(x=>!q||x.toLowerCase().includes(q));
      rows = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{ const r = S.wvSort[cfg.k]==='use'
        ? cfg.use(a)-cfg.use(b) || a.localeCompare(b) : a.localeCompare(b); return r*dir; });
      const nLen = this.state[cfg.listKey].length;
      return {title:cfg.title, sub:cfg.sub, count:nLen+' '+(nLen===1?cfg.noun:cfg.nounPlural),

        onInput:e=>this.set({[cfg.inputKey]:e.target.value}),
        onKey:e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); addW(cfg.inputKey,cfg.listKey,cfg.label); } },
        onAdd:()=>addW(cfg.inputKey,cfg.listKey,cfg.label),
        addStyle:wAddStyle(!!(this.state[cfg.inputKey]||'').trim()),
        q:S.wvQ[cfg.k]||'', onQ:e=>this.set({wvQ:{...S.wvQ,[cfg.k]:e.target.value}}),
        sortKey:S.wvSort[cfg.k]||'name', onSort:e=>this.set({wvSort:{...S.wvSort,[cfg.k]:e.target.value}}),

        dirIcon:S.wvDir[cfg.k]==='desc'?'fa-solid fa-arrow-up-a-z':'fa-solid fa-arrow-down-a-z',
        dirLabel:S.wvDir[cfg.k]==='desc'?'Descending':'Ascending',
        empty:rows.length===0,
        rows:rows.map(x=>({name:x, nameStyle:cfg.chip
            ? 'padding:2px 9px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(201,160,220,.12);border:1px solid rgba(201,160,220,.35);font:600 10px var(--sans);color:var(--tag);min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap'
            : 'font:600 11.5px var(--serif);color:var(--text);min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap',
          use:(n=>n?n+' '+cfg.useNoun+(n===1?'':'s'):'unused')(cfg.use(x)),
          onDel:()=>cfg.onDel(x, cfg.use(x))}))};
    };

        placeholder:'New recipe category', useLabel:'Recipes', useNoun:'recipe', use:wrcatUse,
        onDel:(c,n)=>{ this.setState({wrcats:S.wrcats.filter(x=>x!==c), dirty:true});
          this.flash('Recipe category removed', n ? n+' recipes fall back to uncategorised in every system.' : 'Nothing used it.','fa-solid fa-trash','var(--warning)'); }}),
      cat:wvPanel({k:'cat', listKey:'wcats', inputKey:'wcatInput', title:'Component categories',

          this.setState({wcats:S.wcats.filter(x=>x!==c), wcls, dirty:true});
          this.flash('Category removed', n ? n+' components lose their world category, and '+inh+' inheriting rule sets fall back to their own system list.' : 'Nothing used it.','fa-solid fa-trash','var(--warning)'); }}),
      tag:wvPanel({k:'tag', listKey:'wtags', inputKey:'wtagInput', title:'Component tags', chip:true,

A component takes its category and tags from the world list, and each system layers its own on top. Tags merge \u2014 the world set plus the system set, minus anything muted in that system. Categories cannot merge, so the world value is the default and a system may override it with any category it can see.'};

    const svTagOn = 'flex:0 1 auto;padding:2px 9px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(201,160,220,.12);border:1px solid rgba(201,160,220,.35);font:600 10px var(--sans);color:var(--tag);min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap';

    const tabStrip = (cur, key, items) => items.filter(Boolean).map(t=>({label:t.label, icon:t.icon, badge:t.badge||'',
      badgeStyle: t.badge ? 'flex:0 0 auto;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-width:17px;height:17px;padding:0 5px;border-radius:999px;font:700 9px var(--sans);'+(t.bad?'background:var(--danger-soft);border:1px solid var(--danger-border);color:var(--danger-text)':'background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--text2)') : 'display:none',

      toolProf[k]={...p, [part]:{...p[part], ...o}}; this.setState({toolProf,dirty:true}); };

      return {isCheck:mode==='check', isTool:mode!=='check',
        modeOpts:[{k:'uses',label:'Limited uses',desc:'A fixed number of uses, then it breaks.',icon:'fa-solid fa-hourglass-half'},
                  {k:'chance',label:'Breakage chance',desc:'A % chance to break each use.',icon:'fa-solid fa-percent'},
                  {k:'dice',label:'Dice expression',desc:'Roll a separate breakage check.',icon:'fa-solid fa-dice-d20'}]
          .map(o=>({...o, style:radioCard(b.mode===o.k), dot:radioDot(b.mode===o.k), onClick:()=>set({mode:o.k})})),
        checkOpts:[{k:false,label:'Breakable',desc:'The active check may break this Tool.',icon:'fa-solid fa-hammer'},
                   {k:true,label:'Immune',desc:'This Tool ignores check-driven breakage.',icon:'fa-solid fa-shield-halved'}]
          .map(o=>({...o, style:radioCard(!!b.immune===o.k), dot:radioDot(!!b.immune===o.k), onClick:()=>set({immune:o.k})})),
        showUses:b.mode==='uses', showChance:b.mode==='chance', showDice:b.mode==='dice',
        uses:b.uses, usesLabel:(b.uses===null||b.uses<1||b.uses>=99)?'\u221e':String(b.uses),
        usesNote:(b.uses===null||b.uses<1||b.uses>=99) ? 'Unlimited \u2014 a copy is never spent by use.' : 'A fresh copy starts with this many uses. Each copy tracks its own remaining count.',
        onUsesDown:()=>set({uses:(b.uses===null||b.uses<1)?null:(b.uses-1<1?null:b.uses-1)}),
        onUsesUp:()=>set({uses:(b.uses===null||b.uses<1)?1:Math.min(99,b.uses+1)}),
        chance:b.chance, onChance:e=>set({chance:Math.max(0,Math.min(100,parseInt(e.target.value||'0',10)))}),
        chanceBand: b.chance<=0 ? 'Unbreakable' : b.chance<=10 ? 'Rarely breaks' : b.chance<=30 ? 'Breaks now and then' : b.chance<=60 ? 'Breaks often' : 'Breaks almost every use',
        chanceBandStyle:'font:600 9.5px var(--sans);white-space:nowrap;color:'+(b.chance<=0?'#8FBEF2':b.chance<=10?'#79C295':b.chance<=30?'#C9C05A':b.chance<=60?'#D08C4A':'#D2685F'),
        chanceNote: b.chance<=0 ? 'At 0% the Tool never breaks from use.' : 'Each time the Tool is used, this percentage is its chance to break.',
        formula:b.formula||'', onFormula:e=>set({formula:e.target.value}),
        below:b.below, onBelowDown:()=>set({below:Math.max(-20,(b.below||0)-1)}), onBelowUp:()=>set({below:Math.min(20,(b.below||0)+1)}),
        stepBtn, numField}; };
    const KINDMETA = {comp:{icon:'fa-solid fa-cube', tint:'var(--success)', label:'Component'},
      tag:{icon:'fa-solid fa-tag', tint:'var(--tag)', label:'Tag'},
      ess:{icon:'fa-solid fa-flask-vial', tint:'var(--water)', label:'Essence'},
      cur:{icon:'fa-solid fa-coins', tint:'var(--accent)', label:'Currency'}};
    const segStyle = on => 'padding:4px 9px;font:600 9.5px var(--sans);cursor:pointer;'+(on?'background:rgba(201,160,220,.22);color:var(--text)':'background:transparent;color:var(--subtle)');
    const addChip = 'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:6px 11px;border-radius:8px;border:1px dashed var(--border-strong);font:600 10.5px var(--sans);color:var(--text2);cursor:pointer';
    const altChip = 'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:7px;border:1px dashed var(--accent-border);font:600 10px var(--sans);color:var(--accent);cursor:pointer';
    const uid = () => 'r'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7);
    const blankReq = k => ({id:uid(), kind:k, ref:'', tags:[], pol:'any', qty:1, alts:[]});
    const repCtl = (get, set, scope) => {
      const rows = get().repair || [];
      const put = arr => set({repair:arr});
      const mut = (rid, fn) => put(rows.map(r=>r.id===rid?fn({...r}):r));
      const mutM = (rid, mid, fn) => rid===mid ? mut(rid, fn) : mut(rid, r=>({...r, alts:(r.alts||[]).map(a=>a.id===mid?fn({...a}):a)}));
      const del = (rid, mid) => rid===mid
        ? put(rows.filter(r=>r.id!==rid))
        : mut(rid, r=>({...r, alts:(r.alts||[]).filter(a=>a.id!==mid)}));
      const openK = S.rpOpen, setOpen = k => this.set({rpOpen:S.rpOpen===k?'':k, rpQ:''});
      const liveComp = CAT.filter(c=>!S.gone[c.id]);
      const liveEss = S.ess.filter(e=>!S.essGone[e.id]);
      const member = (m, rid, isAlt) => {
        const K = KINDMETA[m.kind] || KINDMETA.comp, key = scope+':'+m.id;
        const q = (openK===key+':s') ? (S.rpQ||'') : '';
        const pool = m.kind==='ess' ? liveEss.map(e=>({val:e.id,label:e.name,icon:e.icon,tint:e.color}))
                  : m.kind==='cur' ? CURR.map(c=>({val:c.val,label:c.label,icon:'fa-solid fa-coins',tint:'var(--accent)'}))
                                    : liveComp.map(c=>({val:c.id,label:c.name,icon:c.icon,tint:c.ic}));
        const chosen = pool.find(p=>p.val===m.ref);
        const searching = m.kind!=='tag' && (openK===key+':s' || !chosen);
        const sugg = pool.filter(p=>!q||p.label.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())).slice(0,7);
        const tagPool = S.wtags.filter(t=>!(m.tags||[]).includes(t));
        const tq = (openK===key+':t') ? (S.rpQ||'') : '';
        const tagOpts = tagPool.filter(t=>!tq||t.toLowerCase().includes(tq.toLowerCase()));
        return {
          icon:K.icon, chip:'width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:7px;flex:0 0 auto;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);color:'+K.tint,
          rowStyle:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:7px 9px;border-radius:8px;background:'+(isAlt?'var(--bg1)':'var(--bg2)')+';border:1px solid '+(m.kind==='tag'?'rgba(201,160,220,.34)':'var(--border)'),
          kindVal:m.kind, kinds:['comp','tag','ess','cur'].map(k=>({val:k,label:KINDMETA[k].label})),
          onKind:e=>{ const k=e.target.value; mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x, kind:k, ref:'', tags:[], pol:'any'})); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); },
          isTag:m.kind==='tag', isSearch:m.kind!=='tag',
          policyWord:m.pol==='all'?'All of':'Any of',
          anyStyle:segStyle(m.pol!=='all'), allStyle:segStyle(m.pol==='all'),
          onAnyOf:()=>mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,pol:'any'})), onAllOf:()=>mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,pol:'all'})),
          tagChips:(m.tags||[]).map(t=>({name:t, onRemove:()=>mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,tags:x.tags.filter(y=>y!==t)}))})),
          onAddTag:()=>setOpen(key+':t'), tagPickerOpen:openK===key+':t',
          tagQuery:tq, onTagQuery:e=>this.set({rpQ:e.target.value}),
          tagOptions:tagOpts.map(t=>({name:t, onPick:()=>{ mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,tags:[...(x.tags||[]),t]})); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); }})),
          noTagOptions:tagOpts.length===0,
          hasName:!!chosen && !searching, name:chosen?chosen.label:'', nameTint:chosen?chosen.tint:'var(--muted)', nameIcon:chosen?chosen.icon:K.icon,
          onClear:()=>setOpen(key+':s'),
          searching, query:q, onQuery:e=>this.set({rpOpen:key+':s', rpQ:e.target.value}),
          placeholder:m.kind==='ess'?'Search essences\u2026':m.kind==='cur'?'Search currencies\u2026':'Search components\u2026',
          showSuggest:openK===key+':s',
          suggestions:sugg.map(p=>({label:p.label, icon:p.icon, tint:p.tint,
            onPick:()=>{ mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,ref:p.val})); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); }})),
          noMatches:sugg.length===0,
          qty:m.qty, qtyUnit:m.kind==='cur'?(m.ref||''):'',
          onQtyDown:()=>mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,qty:Math.max(1,(x.qty||1)-1)})),
          onQtyUp:()=>mutM(rid,m.id,x=>({...x,qty:Math.min(999,(x.qty||1)+1)})),
          canOr:!isAlt, orOpen:openK===key+':o', onOrToggle:()=>setOpen(key+':o'),
          orOpts:['comp','tag','ess','cur'].map(k=>({label:KINDMETA[k].label, icon:KINDMETA[k].icon, tint:KINDMETA[k].tint,
            style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:7px 8px;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer;font:600 11px var(--sans);color:var(--text2)',
            onPick:()=>{ mut(rid, r=>({...r, alts:[...(r.alts||[]), blankReq(k)]})); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); }})),
          onRemove:()=>{ del(rid,m.id); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); }
        }; };
      return {
        blocks:rows.map(r=>{ const isChoice=(r.alts||[]).length>0;
          return {isChoice,
            shell:isChoice ? 'padding:10px 11px;border-radius:9px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--accent-border)' : '',
            members:[member(r,r.id,false), ...(r.alts||[]).map(a=>member(a,r.id,true))],
            altAdders:['comp','tag','ess','cur'].map(k=>({label:'alt '+KINDMETA[k].label.toLowerCase(), icon:KINDMETA[k].icon, style:altChip,
              onPick:()=>mut(r.id, x=>({...x, alts:[...(x.alts||[]), blankReq(k)]}))}))}; }),
        empty:rows.length===0,
        countLabel:rows.length ? rows.length+' requirement'+(rows.length===1?'':'s') : 'none yet',
        countStyle:'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);font:600 9px var(--sans);color:'+(rows.length?'var(--text2)':'var(--subtle)'),
        adders:['comp','tag','ess','cur'].map(k=>({label:'Add '+KINDMETA[k].label.toLowerCase(), icon:KINDMETA[k].icon, style:addChip,
          onPick:()=>put([...rows, blankReq(k)])})),
        summary:(()=>{ if(!rows.length) return 'Nothing listed \u2014 a broken copy cannot be mended.';
          const one = m => m.kind==='comp' ? ((m.qty>1?m.qty+'\u00d7 ':'')+(((CAT.find(c=>c.id===m.ref)||{}).name)||'unset component'))
            : m.kind==='ess' ? ((m.qty>1?m.qty+'\u00d7 ':'')+(((S.ess.find(e=>e.id===m.ref)||{}).name)||'unset essence')+' essence')
            : m.kind==='cur' ? (m.ref ? m.qty+' '+m.ref : 'unset currency')
            : ((m.tags||[]).length ? (m.qty>1?m.qty+'\u00d7 ':'')+(m.pol==='all'?'all of ':'any of ')+m.tags.join(', ') : 'unset tag');
          return 'Mending consumes '+rows.map(r=>[r,...(r.alts||[])].map(one).join(' or ')).join(' + ')+'.'; })()
      }; };
    const obCtl = (get, set, scope) => { const o=get();
      return {opts:[{k:'destroy',label:'Destroy the item',desc:'The tool is consumed and removed.',icon:'fa-solid fa-trash'},
                    {k:'broken',label:'Mark as broken',desc:'Sets a broken flag; appends \u201c (Broken)\u201d.',icon:'fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation'},

          .map(x=>({...x, style:radioCard(o.onBreak===x.k), dot:radioDot(o.onBreak===x.k), onClick:()=>set({onBreak:x.k})})),
        showRepl:o.onBreak==='replace', repl:o.repl||'',
        ...(()=>{ const rkey=scope+':repl', live=CAT.filter(c=>!S.gone[c.id]), pick=live.find(c=>c.id===o.repl);
          const open=S.rpOpen===rkey, rq=open?(S.rpQ||''):'', drag=S.rpDrag===rkey;
          const sug=live.filter(c=>!rq||c.name.toLowerCase().includes(rq.toLowerCase())).slice(0,7);
          return {hasRepl:!!pick, noRepl:!pick,
            replName:pick?pick.name:'', replSrc:pick?(pick.src+' \u00b7 '+pick.uuid):'', replIcon:pick?pick.icon:'fa-solid fa-cube',
            replChip:'width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9px;flex:0 0 auto;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:13px;background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);color:'+(pick?pick.ic:'var(--subtle)'),
            onReplUnlink:()=>{ set({repl:''}); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); this.flash('Component unlinked','Pick or drop another Component to produce on break.','fa-solid fa-link-slash','var(--warning)'); },
            replDropStyle:'display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:7px;padding:16px 12px;border-radius:10px;border:1.5px dashed '+(drag?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border-strong)')+';background:'+(drag?'var(--accent-soft)':'var(--surface-soft)'),
            replDropIcon:drag?'fa-solid fa-hand-pointer':'fa-solid fa-arrow-down-to-bracket',

            onReplOver:e=>{ e.preventDefault(); if(!drag) this.set({rpDrag:rkey}); },
            onReplLeave:()=>{ if(drag) this.set({rpDrag:''}); },
            onReplDrop:e=>{ e.preventDefault();
              let txt=''; try{ txt=(e.dataTransfer&&e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'))||''; }catch(err){}
              const hit=live.find(c=>txt&&c.name.toLowerCase()===txt.trim().toLowerCase()) || live.find(c=>txt&&txt.toLowerCase().includes(c.name.toLowerCase()));
              this.set({rpDrag:''});
              if(!hit){ this.flash('Nothing recognised','Drag a managed Component out of the catalogue, or use Click to search.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
              set({repl:hit.id}); this.flash(hit.name+' linked','Produced in place of the Tool when it breaks.','fa-solid fa-link'); },

            replQuery:rq, onReplQuery:e=>this.set({rpOpen:rkey, rpQ:e.target.value}),
            replSuggestions:sug.map(c=>({label:c.name, sub:c.src, icon:c.icon, tint:c.ic,
              onPick:()=>{ set({repl:c.id}); this.set({rpOpen:'',rpQ:''}); }})),
            replNoMatches:sug.length===0}; })(),
        showRepair:o.onBreak==='broken', rep:repCtl(get,set,scope)}; };
    const reqCtl = (get, set) => { const r=get(), sel=Object.keys(r.prereqs||{}).filter(k=>r.prereqs[k]);
      return {prereqOn:r.prereqOn, track:svTrack(r.prereqOn), knob:svKnob(r.prereqOn), onToggle:()=>set({prereqOn:!r.prereqOn}),

        failOpts:[{k:'unusable',label:'Tool is unusable',desc:'The character cannot use this Tool for crafting, salvage or gathering.',icon:'fa-solid fa-ban'},

    const toolRules = (tid,sid) => [
      {icon:'fa-solid fa-hourglass-half', label:this.brkLabel(tid,sid), note:this.brkNote(tid,sid)},

    const toolChip = t => 'width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:9px;flex:0 0 auto;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:15px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4a5560,#28313a);color:'+t.ic;

    /* world tools list */
    const tlQ=(S.toolQ||'').trim().toLowerCase(), tlDir=S.toolDir==='desc'?-1:1;
    let tlRows=S.tools.filter(t=>!tlQ||(t.name+' '+t.item).toLowerCase().includes(tlQ));
    tlRows=tlRows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{ let r=0;
      if (S.toolSort==='use') r=this.toolSysList(a.id).length-this.toolSysList(b.id).length||a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (S.toolSort==='break') r=this.brkLabel(a.id,null).localeCompare(this.brkLabel(b.id,null))||a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else r=a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      return r*tlDir; });
    d.tl = {q:S.toolQ, onQ:e=>this.set({toolQ:e.target.value}),
      sortKey:S.toolSort, onSort:e=>this.set({toolSort:e.target.value}),

      modeNote:S.wBreakMode==='check'?'The active check decides breakage \u00b7 world default for every system':'Each Tool tracks its own breakage \u00b7 world default for every system',

          useText:used+' system'+(used===1?'':'s'),
          track:svTrack(w.on), knob:svKnob(w.on),
          onToggle:e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); twdSet(t.id)({on:!w.on}); },

      onOpen:()=>this.set({view:'toolEntry', toolId:tinsT.id, toolTab:'ov'})};

    /* world tool editor */
    const teT = this.tool(S.toolId || S.toolInsp), teW = S.twd[teT.id];

            toolProf[stinsT.id+'|'+S.sysId]={on:true, inh:{brk:true,ob:true,req:true},

    /* system tool rules editor */
    const tpT = this.tool(S.toolProfId || S.stInsp), tpP = this.tpOf(tpT.id,S.sysId), tpE = this.effTool(tpT.id,S.sysId);

        toolProf[k]={...tpP, on:!tpP.on}; this.setState({toolProf,dirty:true}); },

    const sleQ = (S.sleQ||'').trim().toLowerCase();

    let sleRows = sleAll.filter(e=>{

      if (S.sleFilter==='in' && !this.hasEss(e.id,S.sysId)) return false;
      return true; });

    sleRows = sleRows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{ let r=0;
      if (S.sleSort==='comp') r = this.essCompCount(a.id,S.sysId)-this.essCompCount(b.id,S.sysId) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (S.sleSort==='rec') r = this.essRecipeCount(a.id,S.sysId)-this.essRecipeCount(b.id,S.sysId) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (S.sleSort==='state') r = sleState(a)-sleState(b) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else r = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      return r*sleDir; });
    const sleMine = sleAll.filter(e=>this.hasEss(e.id,S.sysId)).length;

    if (S.sbeState) seBits.push(S.sbeState==='on'?'enable':'disable');
    if (S.sbeLink) seBits.push('effect source');
    if (S.sbeMacro) seBits.push('macro');

        ? (seBits.length ? (seBits.length>2 ? 'Edit '+seIds.length+(seIds.length===1?' essence':' essences') : 'Apply '+seBits.join(' + ')+' to '+seIds.length+(seIds.length===1?' essence':' essences'))
                         : 'Stage a change to apply to '+seIds.length+(seIds.length===1?' essence':' essences'))
        : 'Edit essence rules',

      rows:sleRows.map(e=>{ const member=this.hasEss(e.id,S.sysId), p=S.essProf[e.id+'|'+S.sysId], sel=!!S.sesel[e.id];
        return {name:e.name, icon:e.icon, member, notMember:!member,
          box:'width:17px;height:17px;border-radius:5px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;cursor:pointer;font-size:8px;color:var(--on-accent);background:'+(sel?'var(--accent)':'transparent')+';border:1px solid '+(sel?'var(--accent)':'var(--border-strong)'),
          boxIcon:sel?'fa-solid fa-check':'',
          onCheck:ev=>{ev.stopPropagation(); const s2={...S.sesel}; if(s2[e.id]) delete s2[e.id]; else s2[e.id]=1; this.set({sesel:s2});},
          onSelect:()=>this.set({sleInsp:e.id, seiSysQ:'', seiSysPage:0}),
          stats:[{val:member?this.essCompCount(e.id,S.sysId):'\u2014', label:'Components'},{val:member?this.essRecipeCount(e.id,S.sysId):'\u2014', label:'Recipes'}],
          chip:'width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4a5560,#28313a);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:15px;color:'+e.color,
          state: !member ? 'Not in this system' : p.on ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled',
          pill: !member ? pill('var(--surface-soft)','var(--border)','var(--disabled)')
            : p.on ? pill('var(--success-soft)','var(--success-border)','var(--success-text)')
            : pill('var(--surface-soft)','var(--border-strong)','var(--subtle)'),
          summary: member ? this.essSummary(e.id,S.sysId) : (e.desc||'No description yet.'),
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:10px;cursor:pointer;background:'+(S.sleInsp===e.id?'var(--surface-active)':(member?'var(--bg1)':'var(--surface-soft)'))+';border:1px solid '+(S.sleInsp===e.id?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border)')+(member?'':';opacity:.72'),
          onOpen:()=>this.set({view:'essProfile', essProfId:e.id}),
          onAdd:()=>{ const essProf={...S.essProf}; essProf[e.id+'|'+S.sysId]={on:true,inh:{link:true,macro:true},link:null,macro:null,from:null};
            this.setState({essProf,dirty:true}); this.flash('Rules created in '+sys.name, e.name+' can now be carried by components here. Its rules start empty.','fa-solid fa-plus'); }}; })};

    /* ---------------- system essence rules · bulk + inspector ---------------- */
    const seMemCount = seIds.filter(id=>this.hasEss(id,S.sysId)).length, seMissing = seIds.length-seMemCount;
    const seDrop = (kind, hot) => 'display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:7px;padding:16px;border-radius:11px;cursor:copy;text-align:center;border:1.5px dashed '+(hot?'var(--accent)':'var(--border-strong)')+';background:'+(hot?'var(--accent-soft)':'transparent');
    const seParse = (e, list, fallbackIcon) => { e.preventDefault();
      let raw = ''; try { raw = (e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain')||e.dataTransfer.getData('text')||'').trim(); } catch(err) {}
      let uuid = raw, name = '';
      if (raw.charAt(0)==='{') { try { const j=JSON.parse(raw); uuid=j.uuid||j.id||''; name=j.name||''; } catch(err) {} }
      const hit = list.find(x=>x.uuid===uuid || x.name===uuid || x.name===name);
      if (hit) return hit;
      if (!uuid && !name) return null;
      return {name:name||uuid, uuid:uuid||name, effects:'Dropped reference', note:'Dropped reference', icon:fallbackIcon};
    };
    d.sbe = {countLabel:seIds.length+(seIds.length===1?' essence selected':' essences selected'),
      note:'Staged changes are written to '+sys.name+'\u2019s rules only.',
      infoNote:'Names, icons and colours are world definitions and stay per essence. What you change in bulk here is this system\u2019s own rules: whether it is enabled, its effect source and its on-craft macro.',

      memText:seMemCount+' of '+seIds.length+' have rules in '+sys.name,
      hasMissing:seMissing>0, allMembers:seMissing===0,
      missingLabel:'Add the other '+seMissing+' to '+sys.name,

      memNote:seMissing ? 'Staged changes skip the '+seMissing+' without rules here \u2014 add them first and they start enabled.' : 'All of them already carry rules here.',

        onClick:()=>this.set({sbeState:k}),

      linkStaged: S.sbeLink ? (S.sbeLink==='clear'?'unlink':S.sbeLink.name) : 'unchanged',
      linkPicked:!!S.sbeLink, onClearLink:()=>this.set({sbeLink:'', sbeDropL:false}),
      linkHasItem: !!S.sbeLink && S.sbeLink!=='clear', linkIsClear: S.sbeLink==='clear', linkNone: !S.sbeLink,
      linkName: (S.sbeLink && S.sbeLink!=='clear') ? S.sbeLink.name : '',
      linkUuid: (S.sbeLink && S.sbeLink!=='clear') ? S.sbeLink.uuid : '',
      linkEffects: (S.sbeLink && S.sbeLink!=='clear') ? S.sbeLink.effects : '',

      onLinkDrop:e=>{ const it=seParse(e, EFFECT_ITEMS, 'fa-solid fa-wand-sparkles');
        if(!it){ this.set({sbeDropL:false}); this.flash('Nothing recognised','Drag an item out of a compendium or the Items directory.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
        this.set({sbeLink:it, sbeDropL:false});
        this.flash(it.name+' staged','Commit below to write it onto all '+seIds.length+' in '+sys.name+'.','fa-solid fa-link'); },
      onStageLinkClear:()=>this.set({sbeLink:'clear'}),
      linkClearLabel:'Unlink on all '+seIds.length,
      linkNote: S.sbeLink==='clear' ? 'Every selected essence stops transferring active effects in '+sys.name+'.'
        : S.sbeLink ? 'Those active effects are copied onto anything crafted with these essences here.'
        : 'Leave it unchanged, drop one item to set it on all of them, or unlink them all.',
      macroStaged: S.sbeMacro ? (S.sbeMacro==='clear'?'remove':S.sbeMacro.name) : 'unchanged',
      macroPicked:!!S.sbeMacro, onClearMacro:()=>this.set({sbeMacro:'', sbeDropM:false}),
      macroHasItem: !!S.sbeMacro && S.sbeMacro!=='clear', macroIsClear: S.sbeMacro==='clear', macroNone: !S.sbeMacro,
      macroName: (S.sbeMacro && S.sbeMacro!=='clear') ? S.sbeMacro.name : '',
      macroUuid: (S.sbeMacro && S.sbeMacro!=='clear') ? S.sbeMacro.uuid : '',
      macroDetail: (S.sbeMacro && S.sbeMacro!=='clear') ? (S.sbeMacro.note||'') : '',
      macroDropStyle:seDrop('macro', S.sbeDropM),
      macroDropLabel:'Drop a macro here to run it on craft',
      onMacroOver:e=>{ e.preventDefault(); if(!S.sbeDropM) this.set({sbeDropM:true}); },
      onMacroLeave:()=>{ if(S.sbeDropM) this.set({sbeDropM:false}); },
      onMacroDrop:e=>{ const m=seParse(e, MACRO_DOCS, 'fa-solid fa-code');
        if(!m){ this.set({sbeDropM:false}); this.flash('Nothing recognised','Drag a macro out of the Macro directory or a compendium.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
        this.set({sbeMacro:m, sbeDropM:false});
        this.flash(m.name+' staged','Commit below to attach it to all '+seIds.length+' in '+sys.name+'.','fa-solid fa-code'); },
      onStageMacroClear:()=>this.set({sbeMacro:'clear'}),
      macroClearLabel:'Remove the macro on all '+seIds.length,
      macroNote: S.sbeMacro==='clear' ? 'Nothing will run on craft for these essences in '+sys.name+'.'
        : S.sbeMacro ? 'That macro runs on craft for all of them here.'
        : 'Leave it unchanged, drop one macro to set it on all of them, or remove theirs.',
      delLabel: S.sbeArm ? 'Confirm \u2014 remove '+seIds.length+' from '+sys.name : 'Remove '+seIds.length+(seIds.length===1?' essence':' essences')+' from '+sys.name+'\u2026',
      delStyle:'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;height:34px;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;font:700 11px var(--sans);'+(S.sbeArm
        ? 'background:var(--danger-text);border:1px solid var(--danger-text);color:var(--bg0)'
        : 'background:var(--danger-soft);border:1px solid var(--danger-border);color:var(--danger-text)'),
      delNote: S.sbeArm
        ? 'Drops their rules in '+sys.name+' only. World definitions and every other system are untouched.'
        : 'Components here keep the values, but nothing resolves on craft until the essence is added back.',
      onDelete:()=>this.removeSelEssFromSys()};

    const seiE = this.essOf(S.sleInsp || (sleRows[0]||sleAll[0]).id);
    const seiP = S.essProf[seiE.id+'|'+S.sysId];
    const seiMem = this.essSysList(seiE.id);
    const seiPg = this.sysPage(seiMem, (S.seiSysQ||'').trim().toLowerCase(), S.seiSysPage, 'seiSysQ', 'seiSysPage');
    const seiEff = this.effEss(seiE.id, S.sysId);
    d.sei = {name:seiE.name, icon:seiE.icon, desc:seiE.desc||'No description yet.',
      chip:'width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4a5560,#28313a);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:16px;color:'+seiE.color,
      sub:(PALETTE.find(p=>p[0]===seiE.color)||['','Custom'])[1]+' \u00b7 '+(seiP ? this.essCompCount(seiE.id,S.sysId)+' components here' : 'not in '+sys.name),
      member:!!seiP, notMember:!seiP,
      state: !seiP ? 'Not in '+sys.name : seiP.on ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled',
      statePill: !seiP ? pill('var(--surface-soft)','var(--border)','var(--disabled)')
        : seiP.on ? pill('var(--success-soft)','var(--success-border)','var(--success-text)')
        : pill('var(--surface-soft)','var(--border-strong)','var(--subtle)'),
      identityNote:'Name, icon and colour come from the Essence Catalogue, shared with '+Math.max(0,seiMem.length-1)+' other system'+(Math.max(0,seiMem.length-1)===1?'':'s')+'. Everything below belongs to '+sys.name+' alone.',
      linkIcon: seiEff.link ? seiEff.link.icon : 'fa-solid fa-wand-sparkles',
      linkName: seiEff.link ? seiEff.link.name : 'No effect source',
      linkMeta: (seiEff.link ? seiEff.link.effects : 'Nothing is transferred on craft here.')+(seiP?(seiEff.inhLink?' \u00b7 world default':' \u00b7 overridden here'):''),
