      macroMeta: (seiEff.macro ? seiEff.macro.note : 'Nothing runs on craft here.')+(seiP?(seiEff.inhMacro?' \u00b7 world default':' \u00b7 overridden here'):''),
      useText:seiMem.length+' / '+SYS.length, noSystems:seiMem.length===0, noMatch:seiMem.length>0&&seiPg.empty,
      sysQ:S.seiSysQ, onSysQ:seiPg.onQ, sysRange:seiPg.range, sysPageLabel:seiPg.pageLabel,
      sysPrevStyle:seiPg.prevStyle, sysNextStyle:seiPg.nextStyle, onSysPrev:seiPg.onPrev, onSysNext:seiPg.onNext,
      systems:seiPg.rows.map(s=>({name:s.name+(s.id===S.sysId?' \u00b7 here':''), onOpen:()=>this.set({view:'essProfile', sysId:s.id, essProfId:seiE.id})})),
      onDefinition:()=>this.set({view:'essEntry', essEntryId:seiE.id, essDraft:false, essRowQ:'', essFilter:'all'}),
      onAdd:()=>{ const essProf={...S.essProf}; essProf[seiE.id+'|'+S.sysId]={on:true,inh:{link:true,macro:true},link:null,macro:null,from:null};
        this.setState({essProf,dirty:true}); this.flash('Rules created in '+sys.name, seiE.name+' can now be carried by components here.','fa-solid fa-plus'); }};
    d.vTools = S.view==='tools'; d.vToolEntry = S.view==='toolEntry'; d.vSysTools = S.view==='sysTools'; d.vToolProf = S.view==='toolProfile';
    d.vSVocab = S.view==='svocab'; d.vSysEss = S.view==='sysEss'; d.vWVocab = S.view==='wvocab'; d.vEss = S.view==='essences'; d.vEssEntry = S.view==='essEntry'; d.vEssProfile = S.view==='essProfile'; d.vStub = S.view==='stub';
    d.stub = S.stub || {scope:'',label:'',icon:'',title:'',body:''};
    const crumb = arr => arr.filter(Boolean).map((c,i,a)=>({label:c.label, onClick:c.onClick||null, more:i<a.length-1,
      style:'white-space:nowrap;'+(i===a.length-1?'color:var(--text2)':'color:var(--subtle)')+(c.onClick?';cursor:pointer':'')}));
    const cWorld = {label:'World'};
    const cSysRoot = [{label:'Crafting systems'}, {label:sys.name}];
    const cCat = {label:'Component catalogue', onClick:()=>this.set({view:'catalogue'})};
    const cEss = {label:'Essence Catalogue', onClick:()=>this.set({view:'essences'})};
    const cCompRules = {label:'Component Rules', onClick:()=>this.set({view:'system', slInsp:null})};
    const cEssRules = {label:'Essence Rules', onClick:()=>this.set({view:'sysEss'})};
    const crumbFor = {
      catalogue:[cWorld,{label:'Component catalogue'}],
      entry:[cWorld,cCat,{label:(this.cat(S.entryId)||CAT[0]).name}],
      essences:[cWorld,{label:'Essence Catalogue'}],
      essEntry:[cWorld,cEss,{label:(S.ess.find(e=>e.id===S.essEntryId)||S.ess[0]).name}],
      wvocab:[cWorld,{label:'Tags & Categories'}],
      system:[...cSysRoot,{label:'Component Rules'}],
      profile:[...cSysRoot,cCompRules,{label:(this.cat(S.profId)||CAT[0]).name}],
      sysEss:[...cSysRoot,{label:'Essence Rules'}],
      essProfile:[...cSysRoot,cEssRules,{label:(S.ess.find(e=>e.id===S.essProfId)||S.ess[0]).name}],
      svocab:[...cSysRoot,{label:'Tags & Categories'}],
      tools:[cWorld,{label:'Tools Catalogue'}],
      toolEntry:[cWorld,{label:'Tools Catalogue', onClick:()=>this.set({view:'tools'})},{label:this.tool(S.toolId||S.toolInsp).name}],
      sysTools:[...cSysRoot,{label:'Tool Rules'}],
      toolProfile:[...cSysRoot,{label:'Tool Rules', onClick:()=>this.set({view:'sysTools'})},{label:this.tool(S.toolProfId||S.stInsp).name}],
      stub:(S.stub&&S.stub.scope==='World')
        ? [cWorld, S.stub.parent?{label:S.stub.parent}:null, {label:S.stub&&S.stub.label}]
        : [...cSysRoot, (S.stub&&S.stub.parent)?{label:S.stub.parent}:null, {label:S.stub&&S.stub.label}]};
    d.crumb = crumb(crumbFor[S.view] || [cWorld]);

    /* ---------------- catalogue list ---------------- */
    const q = S.clQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    let rows = CAT.filter(c=>{
      if (S.gone[c.id]) return false;
      if (q && !(c.name+' '+c.uuid).toLowerCase().includes(q)) return false;
      if (S.clSrc==='broken'){ if (c.id!=='grif') return false; } else if (S.clSrc!=='all' && c.src!==S.clSrc) return false;
      if (S.clMem==='in' && !this.has(c.id,S.sysId)) return false;
      if (S.clMem==='out' && this.has(c.id,S.sysId)) return false;
      if (S.clMem==='orphan' && this.memberSys(c.id).length) return false;
      return true;
    });
    const clDir = S.clDir==='desc' ? -1 : 1;
    rows = rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{ let r=0;
      if (S.clSort==='use') r = this.memberSys(a.id).length - this.memberSys(b.id).length || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (S.clSort==='src') r = a.src.localeCompare(b.src) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else r = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      return r * clDir; });
    const selIds = Object.keys(S.sel), adding = S.cbMode!=='remove';
    const cbStaged = Object.keys(S.cbSys).filter(sid=>SYS.some(s=>s.id===sid));
    const modeStyle = on => 'flex:1;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:28px;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font:700 11px var(--sans);'+(on?'background:var(--accent);color:var(--on-accent)':'color:var(--muted)');
    const delProfCount = selIds.reduce((n,cid)=>n+this.memberSys(cid).length, 0);
    const delSysCount = SYS.filter(s=>selIds.some(cid=>this.has(cid,s.id))).length;
    const delRecCount = selIds.reduce((n,cid)=>n+this.recipeTotal(cid), 0);
    const delRecSys = SYS.filter(s=>selIds.some(cid=>this.recipeUse(cid,s.id)>0)).length;
    const recPhrase = delRecCount
      ? delRecCount+' recipe'+(delRecCount===1?'':'s')+' across '+delRecSys+' system'+(delRecSys===1?'':'s')+(delRecCount===1?' is':' are')+' disabled'
      : 'no recipes reference them';
    const cbTagAddKeys = Object.keys(S.cbTagAdd), cbTagRemKeys = Object.keys(S.cbTagRem);
    const cbHasCls = !!S.cbCat || cbTagAddKeys.length>0 || cbTagRemKeys.length>0;
    const applyCls = () => { if (!cbHasCls) return [];
      const wcls = {...S.wcls}, bits = [];
      selIds.forEach(cid=>{ const w = wcls[cid] || {cat:'',tags:[]};
        const cat = S.cbCat==='__clear' ? '' : (S.cbCat || w.cat);
        const tags = [...new Set([...w.tags.filter(t=>!S.cbTagRem[t]), ...cbTagAddKeys])];
        wcls[cid] = {...w, cat, tags}; });
      if (S.cbCat) bits.push(S.cbCat==='__clear' ? 'category cleared' : 'category '+S.cbCat);
      if (cbTagAddKeys.length) bits.push(cbTagAddKeys.length+' tag'+(cbTagAddKeys.length===1?'':'s')+' added');
      if (cbTagRemKeys.length) bits.push(cbTagRemKeys.length+' removed');
      this.state.wcls = wcls;
      return bits; };
    const cbCommit = () => {
      if (!cbStaged.length && !cbHasCls) { this.flash('Nothing staged','Tick systems to move, or stage a category or tags, then commit.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
      if (!cbStaged.length) { const bits = applyCls();
        this.setState({wcls:this.state.wcls, sel:{}, cbCat:'', cbTagAdd:{}, cbTagRem:{}, dirty:true});
        this.flash(selIds.length+(selIds.length===1?' component reclassified':' components reclassified'), bits.join(' \u00b7 ')+'. Every system that inherits follows.','fa-solid fa-check-double'); return; }
      let prof = S.prof, n = 0;
      if (adding) { selIds.forEach(cid=>cbStaged.forEach(sid=>{ if(!prof[this.pk(cid,sid)]){ this.state.prof=prof; prof=this.addProf(cid,sid,null); n++; } }));
        if (!n) { this.flash('Nothing to add','Every selected component already has rules in those systems.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
        const bits1 = applyCls();
        this.setState({prof, wcls:this.state.wcls, sel:{}, cbSys:{}, cbCat:'', cbTagAdd:{}, cbTagRem:{}, dirty:true});
        this.flash(n+' rule sets created', ['across '+cbStaged.length+' system'+(cbStaged.length===1?'':'s'), ...bits1].join(' \u00b7 '),'fa-solid fa-plus'); return; }
      prof = {...prof};
      selIds.forEach(cid=>cbStaged.forEach(sid=>{ const k=this.pk(cid,sid); if(prof[k]){ delete prof[k]; n++; } }));
      if (!n) { this.flash('Nothing to remove','None of the selected components has rules in those systems.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
      const bits2 = applyCls();
      this.setState({prof, wcls:this.state.wcls, sel:{}, cbSys:{}, cbCat:'', cbTagAdd:{}, cbTagRem:{}, dirty:true});
      this.flash(n+' rule sets removed', ['components and every other system are untouched', ...bits2].join(' \u00b7 '),'fa-solid fa-arrow-right-from-bracket','var(--warning)');
    };
    const cbPer = 5;
    const pageBtn = on => 'width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:8.5px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);color:'+(on?'var(--text2)':'var(--disabled)')+';cursor:'+(on?'pointer':'not-allowed');
    const paged = (list, pg, key) => { const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(list.length/cbPer)), p = Math.min(pg, pages-1);
      return {rows:list.slice(p*cbPer,(p+1)*cbPer),
        pageLabel:'Page '+(p+1)+' of '+pages,
        range: list.length ? 'Showing '+(p*cbPer+1)+'\u2013'+Math.min(list.length,(p+1)*cbPer)+' of '+list.length : 'Showing 0 of 0',
        prevStyle:pageBtn(p>0), nextStyle:pageBtn(p<pages-1),
        onPrev:()=>{ if(p>0) this.set({[key]:p-1}); }, onNext:()=>{ if(p<pages-1) this.set({[key]:p+1}); }}; };
    const cbSysQ = S.cbSysQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    const cbSysList = SYS.filter(x=>!cbSysQ||x.name.toLowerCase().includes(cbSysQ)).slice().sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    const cbSysPg = paged(cbSysList, S.cbSysPage, 'cbSysPage');
    const cbCatQstr = S.cbCatQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    const cbCatMatch = cbCatQstr ? S.wcats.filter(c=>c.toLowerCase().includes(cbCatQstr)) : S.wcats.slice();
    const cbCatClear = !cbCatQstr || ['no world category','none','clear'].some(l=>l.includes(cbCatQstr));
    const cbCatList = [...(cbCatClear?['__clear']:[]), ...cbCatMatch.sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b))];
    const cbCatPg = paged(cbCatList, S.cbCatPage, 'cbCatPage');
    const cbTagQstr = S.cbTagQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    const cbTagList = (cbTagQstr ? S.wtags.filter(t=>t.toLowerCase().includes(cbTagQstr)) : S.wtags.slice()).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
    const cbTagPg = paged(cbTagList, S.cbTagPage, 'cbTagPage');
    const cbFoot = selIds.length
      ? {label: cbStaged.length
           ? (adding?'Add ':'Remove ')+selIds.length+(selIds.length===1?' component':' components')+(adding?' to ':' from ')+cbStaged.length+(cbStaged.length===1?' system':' systems')+(cbHasCls?' + reclassify':'')
           : (cbHasCls ? 'Reclassify '+selIds.length+(selIds.length===1?' component':' components')
                       : 'Pick systems to '+(adding?'add ':'remove ')+selIds.length+(selIds.length===1?' component':' components')+(adding?' to':' from')),
         style:'width:100%;height:36px;border-radius:9px;font:700 12px var(--sans);cursor:pointer;'+((cbStaged.length||cbHasCls)
           ? (cbStaged.length&&!adding?'border:1px solid var(--danger-border);background:var(--danger-soft);color:var(--danger-text)':'border:1px solid var(--accent-border);background:var(--accent);color:var(--on-accent)')
           : 'border:1px solid var(--border-strong);background:var(--bg1);color:var(--disabled)'),
         action:cbCommit}
      : {label:'Open catalogue entry',

      aliasFieldStyle:'flex:1;min-width:0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;padding:0 9px;height:28px;border-radius:7px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid '+(S.aliasInput.trim()?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border)'),
      aliasAddStyle:'flex:0 0 auto;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;height:28px;padding:0 11px;border-radius:7px;font:600 10px var(--sans);'+(S.aliasInput.trim()
        ? 'background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border-strong);color:var(--text);cursor:pointer'
        : 'background:transparent;border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--disabled);cursor:default'),
      onAliasInput:e=>this.set({aliasInput:e.target.value}),
      onAliasKey:e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter'){ e.preventDefault(); d.en.onAddAlias(); } },
      noAliases:(S.aliases[en.id]||[]).length===0,
      aliases:(S.aliases[en.id]||[]).map((a,i)=>({label:a, onDel:()=>{ const al={...S.aliases}; al[en.id]=al[en.id].filter((_,j)=>j!==i); this.setState({aliases:al,dirty:true}); }})),
      onAddAlias:()=>{ const raw=(this.state.aliasInput||'').trim(); if(!raw) return;
        const al={...S.aliases}; const list=al[en.id]||[];
        if (list.includes(raw)) { this.set({aliasInput:''}); this.flash('Already an alias','That uuid is already matched to this entry.','fa-solid fa-circle-info','var(--info)'); return; }
        al[en.id]=[...list,raw]; this.setState({aliases:al,aliasInput:'',dirty:true});
        this.flash('Alias added','Imports matching this uuid will merge into this entry instead of creating a second one.','fa-solid fa-fingerprint'); },
      showDupe:en.id==='moon', dupeNote:'Another entry (“Moonglass Splinter”) shares this compendium uuid. It carries rules in 2 systems that would move to this entry.',
      onMerge:()=>this.flash('Merge preview','Would show both entries, their rules per system, and which id survives.','fa-solid fa-clone','var(--warning)'),
      onBack:()=>this.set({view:'catalogue'}), onSave:()=>{ this.set({dirty:false}); this.flash('Catalogue entry saved','Identity and source references written. No system rules changed.','fa-solid fa-circle-check'); },
      onAddTo:()=>this.set({modal:'addTo', modalSel:{}, copyFrom:''}),
      q:S.enQ, onQ:e=>this.set({enQ:e.target.value}), countText:enRows.length+' of '+SYS.length+' systems',
      deleteNote: enMem.length ? 'Removes the entry and its rules in '+enMem.length+' systems: '+enMem.slice(0,3).map(s=>s.name).join(', ')+(enMem.length>3?' and '+(enMem.length-3)+' more':'')+'.' : 'No system has rules for this entry, so nothing else is affected.',
      onDelete:()=>this.flash('Delete blocked in prototype', enMem.length+' systems would lose their rules. The real flow lists every one before it proceeds.','fa-solid fa-triangle-exclamation','var(--danger)'),
      filters:[['all','All',SYS.length],['in','With rules',enMem.length],['out','Without',SYS.length-enMem.length]].map(([k,label,n])=>{ const on=S.enFilter===k;
        return {label, n, onClick:()=>this.set({enFilter:k}),
          style:'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;font:600 10.5px var(--sans);'+(on?'background:var(--accent-soft);border:1px solid var(--accent-border);color:var(--accent)':'background:var(--bg1);border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--muted)'),
          nStyle:'font:700 9.5px var(--mono);color:'+(on?'var(--accent)':'var(--subtle)')};
      }),
      rows:enRows.map(s=>{ const member=this.has(en.id,s.id), p=S.prof[this.pk(en.id,s.id)];
        return {name:s.name, mode:MODE[s.mode].label, member, notMember:!member,
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;padding:9px 15px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);background:'+(member?'transparent':'var(--surface-soft)'),
          summary:this.summary(en.id,s.id), summaryColor:member?'var(--muted)':'var(--disabled)',
          copied:!!(p&&p.from), copiedNote:p&&p.from?'copied from '+p.from.sys:'',
          onOpen:()=>this.set({view:'profile', sysId:s.id, profId:en.id}),
          onAdd:()=>{ this.setState({prof:this.addProf(en.id,s.id,null),dirty:true}); this.flash('Rules created in '+s.name, en.name+' is now visible to recipes there. Its mechanics start empty.','fa-solid fa-plus'); },
          onRemove:()=>{ const prof={...S.prof}; delete prof[this.pk(en.id,s.id)]; this.setState({prof,dirty:true});
            this.flash('Removed from '+s.name,'The catalogue entry and every other system are untouched.','fa-solid fa-arrow-right-from-bracket','var(--warning)'); }};
      })};

    /* ---------------- system component list ---------------- */
    const v = {...VOCAB[sys.v], cats:this.svOwn(S.sysId,'cat'), tags:this.svOwn(S.sysId,'tag')};
    const slQ = S.slQ.trim().toLowerCase();
    let mine = CAT.filter(c=>this.has(c.id,S.sysId)).filter(c=>{ const p=S.prof[this.pk(c.id,S.sysId)];
      if (slQ && !(c.name+' '+this.effTags(c.id,S.sysId).all.join(' ')).toLowerCase().includes(slQ)) return false;
      if (S.slCat!=='all' && this.effCat(c.id,S.sysId)!==S.slCat) return false;
      if (S.slEss==='none' && Object.keys(p.ess).length) return false;
      if (S.slEss==='any' && !Object.keys(p.ess).length) return false;
      if (S.slEss!=='all' && S.slEss!=='none' && S.slEss!=='any' && p.ess[S.slEss]===undefined) return false;
      return true; });
    const slDir = S.slDir==='desc' ? -1 : 1;
    mine = mine.slice().sort((a,b)=>{ let r=0;
      if (S.slSort==='cat') r = (this.effCat(a.id,S.sysId)||'').localeCompare(this.effCat(b.id,S.sysId)||'') || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (S.slSort==='ess') r = Object.keys(S.prof[this.pk(a.id,S.sysId)].ess).length - Object.keys(S.prof[this.pk(b.id,S.sysId)].ess).length || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else if (S.slSort==='tag') r = this.effTags(a.id,S.sysId).all.length - this.effTags(b.id,S.sysId).all.length || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      else r = a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      return r*slDir; });
    const groupsOf = list => { if (!S.slGroup) return [{cat:'', count:'', showHeader:false, rows:list}];
      const by = {}; list.forEach(c=>{ const k=this.effCat(c.id,S.sysId)||'Uncategorised'; (by[k]=by[k]||[]).push(c); });
      const keys = Object.keys(by).sort(); if (slDir===-1) keys.reverse();
      return keys.map(k=>({cat:k, count:by[k].length, showHeader:true, rows:by[k]})); };
    const mkRow = c => { const p=S.prof[this.pk(c.id,S.sysId)], ek=Object.keys(p.ess), sel=!!S.psel[c.id];
      return {name:c.name, desc:c.desc, icon:c.icon, salvage:p.on && sys.mode!=='none', noEss:ek.length===0,
        box:'width:17px;height:17px;border-radius:5px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;cursor:pointer;font-size:8px;color:var(--on-accent);background:'+(sel?'var(--accent)':'transparent')+';border:1px solid '+(sel?'var(--accent)':'var(--border-strong)'),
        boxIcon:sel?'fa-solid fa-check':'',
        onCheck:e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); const ps={...S.psel}; if(ps[c.id]) delete ps[c.id]; else ps[c.id]=1; this.set({psel:ps}); },
        style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;background:'+(S.slInsp===c.id?'var(--surface-active)':'var(--bg1)')+';border:1px solid '+(S.slInsp===c.id?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border)'),
        chip:'width:40px;height:40px;border-radius:9px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4a5560,#28313a);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:15px;color:'+c.ic,
        recVal:this.recipeUse(c.id,S.sysId),
        dots:ek.map(k=>{ const e=this.essOf(k);
          return {icon:e.icon, qty:p.ess[k], title:e.name+' '+p.ess[k],
            style:'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:3px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:6px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);font:700 9.5px var(--mono);color:'+e.color}; }),
        onSelect:()=>this.set({slInsp:c.id}),
        onOpen:e=>{e.stopPropagation(); this.set({view:'profile', profId:c.id});}}; };
    const B = S.bulk, bTags = this.svOwn(S.sysId,'tag');
    const setB = o => this.setState({bulk:{...B,...o}});
    const sbIds = Object.keys(S.psel);
    const sbHasWcat = sbIds.some(id=>!!this.wclsOf(id).cat);
    const sbCatQ = (S.sbCatQ||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const sbCatAll = this.catOpts(S.sysId).map(o=>o.val);
    const sbCatBase = (sbCatQ ? sbCatAll.filter(c=>c.toLowerCase().includes(sbCatQ)) : sbCatAll.slice()).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
    const sbCatList = [...((sbHasWcat && (!sbCatQ || 'inherit from world'.includes(sbCatQ)))?['__inherit']:[]), ...sbCatBase];
    const sbCatPg = this.pageOf(sbCatList, S.sbCatPage, 'sbCatPage');
    const sbTagQ = (S.sbTagQ||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const sbTagList = (sbTagQ ? bTags.filter(t=>t.toLowerCase().includes(sbTagQ)) : bTags.slice()).sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b));
    const sbTagPg = this.pageOf(sbTagList, S.sbTagPage, 'sbTagPage');
    const sbTagAddKeys = Object.keys(B.tagAdd), sbTagRemKeys = Object.keys(B.tagRem);
    const sbEssAll = this.sysEss(S.sysId);
    const sbEssQ = (S.sbEssQ||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    const sbEssList = (sbEssQ ? sbEssAll.filter(e=>e.name.toLowerCase().includes(sbEssQ)) : sbEssAll.slice()).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    const sbEssPg = this.pageOf(sbEssList, S.sbEssPage, 'sbEssPage');
    const sbEssKeys = Object.keys(B.ess);
    const sbBits = [];
    if (B.cat) sbBits.push('category');
    if (sbTagAddKeys.length || sbTagRemKeys.length) sbBits.push('tags');
    if (sbEssKeys.length) sbBits.push('essences');
    if (B.sal) sbBits.push('salvage');
    if (B.progOn && sys.mode==='progressive') sbBits.push('DC');
    const sbReset = {psel:{}, bulk:{cat:'',tagAdd:{},tagRem:{},ess:{},sal:'',progOn:false,progDc:12}, sbCatQ:'', sbCatPage:0, sbTagQ:'', sbTagPage:0, sbEssQ:'', sbEssPage:0, sbArm:false};
    const slEssList = this.sysEss(S.sysId);
    d.sl = {q:S.slQ, onQ:e=>this.set({slQ:e.target.value}), cat:S.slCat, onCat:e=>this.set({slCat:e.target.value}), cats:this.catOpts(S.sysId).map(o=>o.val),
      showEssFilter:slEssList.length>0, essFilter:S.slEss, onEssFilter:e=>this.set({slEss:e.target.value}),
      essOpts:[{val:'all',label:'All essences'},{val:'any',label:'Carries any essence'},{val:'none',label:'No essences'},
        ...slEssList.map(e=>({val:e.id,label:e.name}))],
      sortKey:S.slSort, onSort:e=>this.set({slSort:e.target.value}),

      dirLabel:S.slDir==='desc'?'Desc':'Asc',

      track:'width:34px;height:19px;border-radius:999px;cursor:pointer;position:relative;background:'+(S.slGroup?'var(--accent)':'var(--surface-raised)')+';border:1px solid '+(S.slGroup?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border)'),
      knob:'position:absolute;top:2px;'+(S.slGroup?'right:2px':'left:2px')+';width:13px;height:13px;border-radius:50%;background:'+(S.slGroup?'var(--on-accent)':'var(--subtle)'),

      masterBox:'width:17px;height:17px;border-radius:5px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:8px;color:var(--on-accent);background:'+(Object.keys(S.psel).length?'var(--accent)':'transparent')+';border:1px solid '+(Object.keys(S.psel).length?'var(--accent)':'var(--border-strong)'),
      masterIcon:Object.keys(S.psel).length?'fa-solid fa-check':'',
      onMaster:()=>{ if(Object.keys(S.psel).length) this.set({psel:{}}); else { const ps={}; mine.forEach(c=>ps[c.id]=1); this.set({psel:ps}); } },
      footLabel: sbIds.length
        ? (sbBits.length ? (sbBits.length>2 ? 'Edit '+sbIds.length+(sbIds.length===1?' component':' components') : 'Apply '+sbBits.join(' + ')+' to '+sbIds.length+(sbIds.length===1?' component':' components'))
                         : 'Stage a change to apply to '+sbIds.length+(sbIds.length===1?' component':' components'))
        : 'Edit system rules',
      footStyle:'width:100%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:7px;height:36px;border-radius:9px;font:700 12px var(--sans);cursor:pointer;'+((!sbIds.length||sbBits.length)?'border:1px solid var(--accent-border);background:var(--accent);color:var(--on-accent)':'border:1px solid var(--border-strong);background:var(--bg1);color:var(--disabled)'),
      footAction:sbIds.length?()=>this.applyBulk():()=>this.set({view:'profile', profId:(this.cat(S.slInsp)||mine[0]||CAT[0]).id}),
      groups:groupsOf(mine).map(g=>({cat:g.cat, count:g.count, showHeader:g.showHeader, rows:g.rows.map(mkRow)}))};

    d.sb = {count:sbIds.length,
      countLabel:sbIds.length+(sbIds.length===1?' component selected':' components selected'),
      note:'Staged changes are written to '+sys.name+' only.',
      infoNote:'Names, art and source links are world catalogue data and stay per component. What you change in bulk here is this system\u2019s own rules: its category, its tags, essence values and salvage.',
      onClear:()=>this.set(sbReset),
      catStaged: B.cat ? (B.cat==='__inherit'?'inherit':B.cat) : 'unchanged',
      catPicked:!!B.cat, onClearCat:()=>this.setState({sbCatQ:'', sbCatPage:0, bulk:{...B, cat:''}}),
      catQ:S.sbCatQ, onCatQ:e=>this.set({sbCatQ:e.target.value, sbCatPage:0}),
      catRange:sbCatPg.range, catPageLabel:sbCatPg.pageLabel,
      catEmpty:sbCatList.length===0, catEmptyText:'Nothing matches that search.',

      onCatPrev:sbCatPg.onPrev, onCatNext:sbCatPg.onNext,
      catRows:sbCatPg.rows.map(c=>{ const on=B.cat===c, inh=c==='__inherit';
        const carried = inh ? sbIds.filter(id=>!!this.wclsOf(id).cat).length : sbIds.filter(id=>this.effCat(id,S.sysId)===c).length;
        return {name: inh?'Inherit from world':c, icon: on?'fa-solid fa-circle-check':'fa-regular fa-circle',
          meta:carried+'/'+sbIds.length, metaStyle:'margin-left:auto;flex:0 0 auto;white-space:nowrap;font:600 9px var(--mono);color:var(--subtle)',
          onClick:()=>setB({cat:on?'':c}),
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:6px 9px;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font:600 10.5px var(--sans);'+(on
            ? 'background:var(--accent-soft);border:1px solid var(--accent-border);color:var(--accent)'
            : 'background:var(--bg1);border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--text2)')}; }),

        : 'Pick one to set it here on every selected component, or leave it unchanged.',
      noTags:bTags.length===0,
      tagStaged:(()=>{ const a=sbTagAddKeys.length, r=sbTagRemKeys.length; if(!a&&!r) return 'unchanged';

      tagAny:sbTagAddKeys.length>0||sbTagRemKeys.length>0,

        name:t, icon:add?'fa-solid fa-plus':'fa-solid fa-minus', flipTitle:add?'Switch to remove':'Switch to add',
        onFlip:()=>{ const A={...B.tagAdd}, R={...B.tagRem}; if(add){ delete A[t]; R[t]=1; } else { delete R[t]; A[t]=1; } setB({tagAdd:A, tagRem:R}); },
        onClear:e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); const A={...B.tagAdd}, R={...B.tagRem}; delete A[t]; delete R[t]; setB({tagAdd:A, tagRem:R}); },
        style:'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:4px 9px;border-radius:999px;font:600 10px var(--sans);'+(add
          ? 'background:var(--success-soft);border:1px solid var(--success-border);color:var(--success-text)'
          : 'background:var(--danger-soft);border:1px solid var(--danger-border);color:var(--danger-text)')})),
      tagQ:S.sbTagQ, onTagQ:e=>this.set({sbTagQ:e.target.value, sbTagPage:0}),
      tagRange:sbTagPg.range, tagPageLabel:sbTagPg.pageLabel,
      tagEmpty:sbTagList.length===0, tagEmptyText: bTags.length ? 'Nothing matches that search.' : sys.name+' defines no component tags.',

      onTagPrev:sbTagPg.onPrev, onTagNext:sbTagPg.onNext,

        const carried = sbIds.filter(id=>this.effTags(id,S.sysId).all.includes(t)).length;
        return {name:t, icon: st==='add'?'fa-solid fa-plus':st==='rem'?'fa-solid fa-minus':'fa-solid fa-tag',

          onClick:()=>{ const A={...B.tagAdd}, R={...B.tagRem};
            if (st==='none') A[t]=1; else if (st==='add') { delete A[t]; R[t]=1; } else delete R[t];
            setB({tagAdd:A, tagRem:R}); },
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:6px 9px;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font:600 10.5px var(--sans);'+(st==='add'
            ? 'background:var(--success-soft);border:1px solid var(--success-border);color:var(--success-text)'
            : st==='rem' ? 'background:var(--danger-soft);border:1px solid var(--danger-border);color:var(--danger-text)'

      tagNote:'These are '+sys.name+'\u2019s own tags. Click once to add, again to remove, again to clear. World tags merge in on top of them.',

        return {name:e.name, icon:e.icon, qty: q>0 ? String(q) : 'removed',

      onEssPrev:sbEssPg.onPrev, onEssNext:sbEssPg.onNext,

        const carried = sbIds.filter(id=>{ const p=S.prof[this.pk(id,S.sysId)]; return p && p.ess[e.id]!==undefined; }).length;
        return {name:e.name, icon:e.icon,

            : 'background:var(--bg1);border:1px solid var(--border)'),

    let mRows = [];
    if (mk==='bulkAdd' || mk==='addTo' || mk==='copyTo') mRows = SYS.map(s=>{
      const already = mk==='bulkAdd' ? Object.keys(S.sel).filter(cid=>this.has(cid,s.id)).length+' of '+Object.keys(S.sel).length+' already here'
        : this.has((mk==='addTo'?en.id:pc.id), s.id) ? 'already has rules' : 'no rules yet';
      return {id:s.id, name:s.name, meta:MODE[s.mode].label+' · '+this.sysEss(s.id).length+' essences', tag:already}; });
    if (mk==='addFrom') mRows = CAT.filter(c=>!this.has(c.id,S.sysId)).map(c=>({id:c.id, name:c.name, meta:c.src, tag:this.memberSys(c.id).length+' other systems'}));
    if (mk==='yield') mRows = CAT.filter(c=>c.id!==pc.id).map(c=>({id:c.id, name:c.name, meta:c.src, tag:this.has(c.id,S.sysId)?'has rules here':'no rules in '+sys.name}));
    if (mk==='essAddTo') mRows = SYS.map(s=>({id:s.id, name:s.name, meta:this.sysEss(s.id).length+' essences', tag:this.hasEss(ed.id,s.id)?'already has rules':'no rules yet'}));
    if (mk==='essBulkAdd') mRows = SYS.map(s=>({id:s.id, name:s.name, meta:this.sysEss(s.id).length+' essences', tag:Object.keys(S.esel).filter(id=>this.hasEss(id,s.id)).length+' of '+Object.keys(S.esel).length+' already here'}));
    if (mk==='essCopyTo') mRows = SYS.filter(s=>s.id!==S.sysId).map(s=>({id:s.id, name:s.name, meta:this.sysEss(s.id).length+' essences', tag:this.hasEss(ep.id,s.id)?'will be overwritten':'no rules yet'}));
    if (mk==='essLink'||mk==='essLinkW') mRows = EFFECT_ITEMS.map(i=>({id:i.uuid, name:i.name, meta:i.uuid, tag:i.effects.split(' · ')[0]}));
    if (mk==='essMacro'||mk==='essMacroW') mRows = MACRO_DOCS.map(m=>({id:m.uuid, name:m.name, meta:m.uuid, tag:m.note}));
    const copyOpts = this.memberSys(en.id).map(s=>({val:s.id,label:'Copy of '+s.name+'\u2019s rules'}));
    d.modal = {open:!!mk, stop:e=>e.stopPropagation(), onClose:()=>this.set({modal:null}),
      showCopy:(mk==='addTo'||mk==='essAddTo') && copyOpts.length>0, copyFrom:S.copyFrom,
      copyOpts: mk==='essAddTo' ? this.essSysList(ed.id).map(s=>({val:s.id,label:'Copy of '+s.name+'\u2019s rules'})) : copyOpts,
      onCopyFrom:e=>this.set({copyFrom:e.target.value}),
      title: mk==='bulkAdd' ? 'Add '+Object.keys(S.sel).length+' entries to systems' : mk==='addTo' ? 'Add '+en.name+' to systems' : mk==='addFrom' ? 'Add from catalogue to '+sys.name : mk==='copyTo' ? 'Copy these rules to other systems'
        : mk==='essBulkAdd' ? 'Add '+Object.keys(S.esel).length+' essences to systems'
        : mk==='essAddTo' ? 'Add '+ed.name+' to systems' : mk==='essCopyTo' ? 'Copy these essence rules to other systems' : mk==='essLink'||mk==='essLinkW' ? 'Pick an active effect source' : mk==='essMacro'||mk==='essMacroW' ? 'Attach a macro' : 'Pick a yield',
      sub: mk==='copyTo' ? 'Each destination gets its own copy. Values its vocabulary does not have — tags, essences, categories — are dropped, not invented.'
        : mk==='addTo' ? 'A new set of rules appears in each system you pick. Starting from another system\u2019s rules copies its mechanics once.'
        : mk==='yield' ? 'Picking something with no rules in '+sys.name+' will offer to create one — a yield that does not exist here cannot resolve.'
        : mk==='essAddTo' ? 'Each system you pick gets its own essence rules. Copying one brings its effect source and macro across once.'
        : mk==='essCopyTo' ? 'Writes this effect source and macro into each destination\u2019s own rules.'
        : mk==='essLinkW' ? 'Becomes the world default. Every system that inherits copies these effects onto anything crafted with this essence.'
        : mk==='essMacroW' ? 'Becomes the world default macro, running on craft in every system that inherits it.'
        : mk==='essLink' ? 'Every active effect on the item is copied onto anything crafted with this essence in '+sys.name+'.'
        : mk==='essMacro' ? 'The macro runs against the crafted item data before it reaches the character.'
        : 'New rules start empty. Nothing is inherited from the catalogue.',
      applyLabel: mk==='copyTo'||mk==='essCopyTo' ? 'Copy rules' : mk==='yield' ? 'Add yield' : mk==='essLink'||mk==='essLinkW' ? 'Link item' : mk==='essMacro'||mk==='essMacroW' ? 'Attach macro' : 'Create rules',
      footNote: Object.keys(S.modalSel).length+' selected',
      rows:mRows.map(r=>{ const on=!!S.modalSel[r.id];
        return {name:r.name, meta:r.meta, tag:r.tag,
          style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;cursor:pointer;background:'+(on?'var(--surface-active)':'var(--bg2)')+';border:1px solid '+(on?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border)'),
          box:'width:17px;height:17px;border-radius:5px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:0 0 auto;font-size:8px;color:var(--on-accent);background:'+(on?'var(--accent)':'transparent')+';border:1px solid '+(on?'var(--accent)':'var(--border-strong)'),
          boxIcon:on?'fa-solid fa-check':'',
          onClick:()=>{ const single = mk==='yield'||mk==='essLink'||mk==='essMacro'||mk==='essLinkW'||mk==='essMacroW'; const ms = single?{}:{...S.modalSel}; if(S.modalSel[r.id]) delete ms[r.id]; else ms[r.id]=1; this.set({modalSel:ms}); }}; }),
      onApply:()=>{
        const picks = Object.keys(S.modalSel);
        if (!picks.length) { this.set({modal:null}); return; }
        if (mk==='bulkAdd'){ let prof=S.prof, n=0;
          Object.keys(S.sel).forEach(cid=>picks.forEach(sid=>{ if(!prof[this.pk(cid,sid)]){ this.state.prof=prof; prof=this.addProf(cid,sid,null); n++; } }));
          this.setState({prof,modal:null,sel:{},dirty:true}); this.flash(n+' rule sets created','Each one starts empty in its own system\u2019s vocabulary.','fa-solid fa-plus'); return; }
        if (mk==='addTo'){ let prof=S.prof, n=0;
          picks.forEach(sid=>{ if(!prof[this.pk(en.id,sid)]){ this.state.prof=prof; prof=this.addProf(en.id,sid,S.copyFrom||null); n++; } });
          this.setState({prof,modal:null,dirty:true}); this.flash(en.name+' added to '+n+' systems', S.copyFrom? 'Copied from '+this.sys(S.copyFrom).name+'. Values that system\u2019s vocabulary does not share were dropped.':'Rules start empty.','fa-solid fa-plus'); return; }
        if (mk==='addFrom'){ let prof=S.prof;
          picks.forEach(cid=>{ this.state.prof=prof; prof=this.addProf(cid,S.sysId,null); });
          this.setState({prof,modal:null,dirty:true}); this.flash(picks.length+' added to '+sys.name,'They are now visible to this system\u2019s recipes.','fa-solid fa-plus'); return; }
        if (mk==='copyTo'){ let prof=S.prof, n=0;
          picks.forEach(sid=>{ this.state.prof=prof; prof=this.addProf(pc.id,sid,S.sysId); n++; });
          this.setState({prof,modal:null,dirty:true}); this.flash('Copied into '+n+' systems','Each is now an independent set of rules. Later edits here stay here.','fa-solid fa-copy'); return; }
        if (mk==='essBulkAdd'){ const essProf={...S.essProf}; let n=0;
          Object.keys(S.esel).forEach(eid=>picks.forEach(sid=>{ const k=eid+'|'+sid; if(!essProf[k]){ essProf[k]={on:true,inh:{link:true,macro:true},link:null,macro:null,from:null}; n++; } }));
          this.setState({essProf,modal:null,esel:{},dirty:true}); this.flash(n+' essence rule sets created','Each starts with no effect source and no macro.','fa-solid fa-plus'); return; }
        if (mk==='essAddTo'){ const essProf={...S.essProf}; const src=S.copyFrom?essProf[ed.id+'|'+S.copyFrom]:null; let n=0;
          picks.forEach(sid=>{ if(!essProf[ed.id+'|'+sid]){ essProf[ed.id+'|'+sid]= src?{...src,from:{sys:this.sys(S.copyFrom).name,date:'today'}}:{on:true,inh:{link:true,macro:true},link:null,macro:null,from:null}; n++; } });
          this.setState({essProf,modal:null,dirty:true});
          this.flash(ed.name+' added to '+n+' systems', S.copyFrom?'Copied from '+this.sys(S.copyFrom).name+'. Each copy is independent.':'Each set of rules starts with no effect source and no macro.','fa-solid fa-plus'); return; }
        if (mk==='essCopyTo'){ const essProf={...S.essProf}; const src=essProf[ep.id+'|'+S.sysId];
          picks.forEach(sid=>{ essProf[ep.id+'|'+sid]={...src, from:{sys:sys.name,date:'today'}}; });
          this.setState({essProf,modal:null,dirty:true}); this.flash('Copied into '+picks.length+' systems','Each is now an independent set of rules.','fa-solid fa-copy'); return; }
        if (mk==='essLinkW'){ const item=EFFECT_ITEMS.find(i=>i.uuid===picks[0]); this.setEssW(ed.id,{link:item});
          this.setState({modal:null}); this.flash(item.name+' set as the default','Every system inheriting the default now transfers its effects.','fa-solid fa-link'); return; }
        if (mk==='essMacroW'){ const m=MACRO_DOCS.find(x=>x.uuid===picks[0]); this.setEssW(ed.id,{macro:m});
          this.setState({modal:null}); this.flash(m.name+' set as the default','Runs on craft in every system inheriting the default.','fa-solid fa-code'); return; }
        if (mk==='essLink'){ const item=EFFECT_ITEMS.find(i=>i.uuid===picks[0]); this.setEssProf(ep.id,S.sysId,{link:item});
          this.setState({modal:null}); this.flash(item.name+' linked','Its active effects now transfer in '+sys.name+' only.','fa-solid fa-link'); return; }
        if (mk==='essMacro'){ const m=MACRO_DOCS.find(x=>x.uuid===picks[0]); this.setEssProf(ep.id,S.sysId,{macro:m});
          this.setState({modal:null}); this.flash(m.name+' attached','Runs on craft in '+sys.name+' only.','fa-solid fa-code'); return; }
        if (mk==='yield'){ const y=this.cat(picks[0]); const gi=S.groupIdx;
          let prof={...S.prof};
          if (!prof[this.pk(y.id,S.sysId)]) { this.state.prof=prof; prof=this.addProf(y.id,S.sysId,null); }
          const key=this.pk(pc.id,S.sysId), cur=prof[key];
          if (sys.mode==='progressive') prof[key]={...cur, results:[...cur.results,{id:y.id,name:y.name,qty:1}]};
          else { const groups=cur.groups.map((g,i)=> i===(gi||0) ? {...g,results:[...g.results,{id:y.id,name:y.name,qty:1}]} : g); prof[key]={...cur,groups}; }
          this.setState({prof,modal:null,groupIdx:null,dirty:true});
          this.flash(y.name+' added as a yield', this.has(y.id,S.sysId)?'It already had rules in '+sys.name+'.':'It had no rules here, so one was created — otherwise the salvage could not resolve.','fa-solid fa-wand-magic-sparkles'); return; }
        this.set({modal:null});
      }};

    const T = S.toast;
    d.toast = {open:!!T, msg:T?T.msg:'', sub:T?T.sub:'', icon:T?T.icon:'', iconStyle:'font-size:14px;color:'+(T?T.color:'var(--success)'), onClose:()=>this.set({toast:null})};
    /* ---------------- player previews ---------------- */
    const pvTile = (icon, color, qty, name, dim, badge) => ({icon, name, qty:'\u00d7'+qty,
      tile:'position:relative;width:100%;aspect-ratio:1;border-radius:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:28px;background:radial-gradient(120% 120% at 30% 20%,#2B3642 0%,#131B23 100%);border:1px solid '+(dim?'var(--border)':'var(--border-strong)')+';color:'+color+';opacity:'+(dim?'.5':'1'),
      qtyStyle:'position:absolute;top:6px;right:6px;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:6px;background:rgba(0,0,0,.55);border:1px solid var(--border);font:700 9px var(--mono);color:var(--text2)',
      badge:badge||'',
      badgeStyle:badge?'position:absolute;bottom:6px;left:6px;padding:1px 7px;border-radius:6px;background:var(--danger-soft);border:1px solid var(--danger-border);font:700 8.5px var(--sans);color:var(--danger-text)':'display:none',
      nameStyle:'font:600 11px var(--sans);text-align:center;margin-top:6px;color:'+(dim?'var(--muted)':'var(--text)')});
    const pvRow = (icon, label, note, badge) => ({icon, label, note:note||'', badge:badge||'',
      iconStyle:'width:28px;height:28px;border-radius:8px;flex:0 0 auto;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);color:var(--accent)',
      noteStyle:note?'font:400 9.5px/1.4 var(--sans);color:var(--subtle);margin-top:2px':'display:none',
      badgeStyle:badge?'flex:0 0 auto;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:var(--surface-soft);border:1px solid var(--border);font:600 9px var(--sans);color:var(--text2)':'display:none'});
    const pvLive = 'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;padding:9px 11px;border-radius:9px;background:var(--bg1);border:1px solid var(--border);font:400 10px/1.4 var(--sans);color:var(--muted)';
    const essPv = (eid, sid) => { const e=this.essOf(eid), p=sid?S.essProf[eid+'|'+sid]:null;
      const pk=sid||S.sysId;
      const carrier = CAT.find(c=>{ const pr=S.prof[this.pk(c.id,pk)]; return pr&&pr.ess&&pr.ess[eid]!==undefined; }) || CAT[0];
      const carrierQty = (()=>{ const pr=S.prof[this.pk(carrier.id,pk)]; return (pr&&pr.ess&&pr.ess[eid])||1; })();
      const wdef = this.essWOf(eid);
      const behaviour = !sid
        ? [pvRow('fa-solid fa-cubes','Counted as an input','Recipes can require a quantity of it, and a disabled essence still matches and is consumed.'),
           pvRow('fa-solid fa-wand-sparkles', wdef.link?'Default effects from '+wdef.link.name:'No default effect source', wdef.link?'Systems that inherit copy these onto anything crafted with it.':'Systems that inherit transfer nothing on craft.'),
           pvRow('fa-solid fa-code', wdef.macro?'Default macro '+wdef.macro.name:'No default macro', wdef.macro?'Runs on craft in every system that inherits.':'Nothing runs on craft where the default is inherited.')]
        : !p ? [pvRow('fa-solid fa-eye-slash','Invisible in this system','No rules here, so nothing in '+this.sys(sid).name+' reads its values.')]
        : p.on ? (()=>{ const ef=this.effEss(eid,sid);
            return [pvRow('fa-solid fa-wand-sparkles', ef.link?'Effects from '+ef.link.name:'No effect source', (ef.link?'Copied onto anything crafted with it here.':'Crafted items gain nothing from it here.')+(ef.inhLink?' Inherited from the world default.':' Overridden here.')),
              pvRow('fa-solid fa-code', ef.macro?'Macro '+ef.macro.name:'No macro', (ef.macro?'Runs against the item data before it reaches the inventory.':'Nothing runs on craft.')+(ef.inhMacro?' Inherited from the world default.':' Overridden here.'))]; })()
        : [pvRow('fa-solid fa-power-off','Disabled here','Values still match ingredients and are consumed, but nothing runs on craft.')];
      return {tileLabel:'As an inventory tile', compLabel:'On a component',
        tile:pvTile(e.icon, e.color, 1, e.name),
        comp:{...pvTile(carrier.icon, carrier.ic, 1, carrier.name),
          essIcon:e.icon,
          essChipStyle:'position:absolute;bottom:6px;left:6px;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:10px;background:rgba(8,12,16,.82);border:1px solid var(--border-strong);color:'+e.color,
          essTitle:'\u00d7'+carrierQty+' '+e.name},
        behaviour, live:'This preview updates live as you edit.'}; };
    const compPv = (cid, sid) => { const c=this.cat(cid), r=hash(cid+(sid||'w'));
      const tags = sid ? this.effTags(cid,sid).all : this.wclsOf(cid).tags;
      const used = RNAMES.filter((_,i)=>((r>>i)&1)===1).slice(0,2);
      const made = RNAMES.filter((n,i)=>!used.includes(n) && ((r>>(i+2))&1)===1).slice(0,1);
      const salv = SALVSRC.filter((_,i)=>((r>>(i+1))&1)===1).slice(0,3);
      return {tileLabel:'As an inventory tile',
        tile:pvTile(c.icon, c.ic, 1+(r%3), c.name),
        art:'Art, name and description come from the linked Item.',
        tags:tags.slice(0,4).map(t=>({label:t, style:'display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(201,160,220,.12);border:1px solid rgba(201,160,220,.35);font:600 9px var(--sans);color:var(--tag)'})),
        catLabel:(sid?this.effCat(cid,sid):this.wclsOf(cid).cat)||'Uncategorised',
        scopeNote: sid ? 'What a player sees in '+this.sys(sid).name+'.' : 'Across every system that has rules for it.',
        usedBy:used.map(n=>pvRow('fa-solid fa-scroll', n, '', 'Ingredient')),
        noUsed:used.length===0,
        producedBy:[...made.map(n=>pvRow('fa-solid fa-scroll', n, '', 'Recipe')),
                    ...salv.map(n=>pvRow('fa-solid fa-recycle', n, '', 'Salvage'))],
        noProduced:made.length+salv.length===0,
        live:'This preview updates live as you edit.'}; };
    const toolPv = (tid, sid) => { const t=this.tool(tid), e=this.effTool(tid,sid);
      const actor = ACTORS.find(a=>a.id===S.pvActor) || null;
      const active = (e.req.prereqOn ? PREREQ.filter(p=>(e.req.prereqs||{})[p.id]) : []);
      const met = p => S.pvPre[p.id]===undefined ? !!(actor && actor.pre[p.id]) : !!S.pvPre[p.id];
      const allMet = active.every(met);
      const bonusRaw = (e.req.bonusOn && (e.req.bonus||'').trim()) ? e.req.bonus.trim() : '';
      const numeric = bonusRaw && !isNaN(Number(bonusRaw));
      const bonusVal = !bonusRaw ? null : numeric ? Number(bonusRaw) : this.modValue(actor, bonusRaw);
      const withheld = !allMet && e.req.prereqFail==='withhold';
      const unusable = !allMet && e.req.prereqFail==='unusable';
      const applied = bonusRaw && allMet && bonusVal!==null;
      return {tileLabel:'As an inventory tile',
        tile:pvTile(t.icon, t.ic, 1, S.pvBroken ? t.name+' (Broken)' : t.name, S.pvBroken, S.pvBroken?'Broken':''),
        brokenLabel:'Show as broken', brokenTrack:svTrack(S.pvBroken), brokenKnob:svKnob(S.pvBroken),
        onBroken:()=>this.set({pvBroken:!S.pvBroken}),
        brokenNote:S.pvBroken
          ? (e.ob.onBreak==='destroy' ? 'With destroy-on-break the tile is gone from the inventory entirely \u2014 shown here dimmed for comparison.'
            : e.ob.onBreak==='broken' ? 'Marked broken: the name gains \u201c (Broken)\u201d and recipes stop accepting it.'
            : 'Replaced by the chosen Component, which can be used in a repair route.')
          : 'A working copy. Recipes and gathering tasks accept it.',
        usesChip: e.mode==='check' ? (e.brk.immune?'Never breaks':'Breaks on the check') : e.brk.mode==='uses' ? ((e.brk.uses===null||e.brk.uses<1||e.brk.uses>=99)?'Unlimited uses':e.brk.uses+(e.brk.uses===1?' use left':' uses left')) : e.brk.mode==='dice' ? e.brk.formula+' \u2264 '+e.brk.below+' breaks it' : (e.brk.chance<=0?'Never breaks from use':e.brk.chance+'% per use'),
        actorLabel:'Preview as',
        actorName:actor?actor.name:'No actor', actorIcon:actor?actor.icon:'fa-solid fa-user-slash',
        pickerOpen:!!S.pvOpen, chevron:S.pvOpen?'fa-solid fa-chevron-up':'fa-solid fa-chevron-down',
        triggerStyle:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;width:100%;height:34px;padding:0 11px;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;font:600 11.5px var(--sans);color:var(--text);background:var(--bg1);border:1px solid '+(S.pvOpen?'var(--accent-border)':'var(--border-strong)'),
        onToggleActor:()=>this.set({pvOpen:!S.pvOpen, pvQ:''}),
        onCloseActor:()=>this.set({pvOpen:false}),
        actorQuery:S.pvQ||'', onActorQuery:ev=>this.set({pvQ:ev.target.value}),
        actorRows:(()=>{ const q=(S.pvQ||'').trim().toLowerCase();
          const list=[{id:'',name:'No actor',icon:'fa-solid fa-user-slash'},...ACTORS].filter(a=>!q||a.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
          return list.map(a=>({label:a.name, icon:a.icon,
            chip:'width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:7px;flex:0 0 auto;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:11px;background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border);color:'+(a.id?'var(--accent)':'var(--subtle)'),
            style:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:7px;cursor:pointer;font:500 11.5px var(--sans);color:'+(S.pvActor===a.id?'var(--text)':'var(--text2)')+';background:'+(S.pvActor===a.id?'var(--surface-active)':'transparent'),
            onClick:()=>this.set({pvActor:a.id, pvPre:{}, pvOpen:false, pvQ:''})})); })(),
        noActors:(()=>{ const q=(S.pvQ||'').trim().toLowerCase();
          return q ? ![{name:'No actor'},...ACTORS].some(a=>a.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) : false; })(),
        hasPrereq:active.length>0, noPrereq:active.length===0,
        prereqRows:active.map(p=>{ const on=met(p);
          return {label:p.label, expr:p.expr,
            state:on?'Met':'Unmet',
            stateStyle:'flex:0 0 auto;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;font:600 9px var(--sans);'+(on?'background:var(--success-soft);border:1px solid var(--success-border);color:var(--success)':'background:var(--danger-soft);border:1px solid var(--danger-border);color:var(--danger-text)'),
            track:svTrack(on), knob:svKnob(on),
            onToggle:()=>this.set({pvPre:{...S.pvPre,[p.id]:!on}})}; }),
        outcome: !actor ? (bonusRaw ? 'Pick a character to resolve @'+bonusRaw : 'Usable, with no check bonus')
          : unusable ? actor.name+' cannot use this Tool'
          : withheld ? 'Present, but the bonus is withheld'
          : applied ? 'Adds '+(bonusVal>=0?'+':'')+bonusVal+' to the check'
          : bonusRaw ? 'Bonus @'+bonusRaw+' does not resolve for '+actor.name
          : 'Usable, with no check bonus',
        outcomeStyle:'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;padding:12px 13px;border-radius:11px;background:'+(!actor?'var(--bg1)':unusable?'var(--danger-soft)':withheld?'var(--warning-soft)':applied?'var(--success-soft)':'var(--bg1)')+';border:1px solid '+(!actor?'var(--border)':unusable?'var(--danger-border)':withheld?'var(--warning-border)':applied?'var(--success-border)':'var(--border)'),
        outcomeIcon: !actor?'fa-solid fa-circle-info':unusable?'fa-solid fa-ban':withheld?'fa-solid fa-eye-slash':applied?'fa-solid fa-plus':'fa-solid fa-circle-info',
        requiredFor:TASKS.filter((_,i)=>((hash(tid+'task')>>i)&1)===1).slice(0,3).map(n=>pvRow('fa-solid fa-seedling', n, '', 'Gathering')),
        noRequired:TASKS.filter((_,i)=>((hash(tid+'task')>>i)&1)===1).length===0,
        live:'This preview updates live as you edit.'}; };
    d.te.pv = toolPv(this.tool(S.toolId||S.toolInsp).id, null);
    d.tp.pv = toolPv(this.tool(S.toolProfId||S.stInsp).id, S.sysId);
    if (d.en) d.en.pv = compPv(S.entryId || CAT[0].id, null);
    if (d.pr) d.pr.pv = compPv(S.profId || CAT[0].id, S.sysId);
    if (d.ee) d.ee.pv = essPv(S.essEntryId || S.ess[0].id, null);
    if (d.ep) d.ep.pv = essPv(S.essProfId || S.ess[0].id, S.sysId);
    d.pvLiveStyle = pvLive;

    /* ---------------- validation tabs on component + essence editors ---------------- */
    const attachVal = (obj, iss, tabKey, cur, mainLabel, mainIcon) => { if(!obj) return;
      obj.val = valPanel(iss); obj.tabMain = cur!=='val'; obj.tabVal = cur==='val';
      obj.tabs = tabStrip(cur, tabKey, [{k:'main', label:mainLabel, icon:mainIcon},
        {k:'val', label:'Validation', icon:'fa-solid fa-clipboard-check',
         badge: iss.block.length ? String(iss.block.length) : '\u2713', bad: iss.block.length>0}]);
      const prev = obj.onSave; if (prev) obj.onSave = guardSave(iss, '', prev); };
    attachVal(d.en, this.compIssues(S.entryId || CAT[0].id, null), 'enTab', S.enTab, 'Catalogue entry', 'fa-solid fa-cube');
    attachVal(d.pr, this.compIssues(S.profId || CAT[0].id, S.sysId), 'prTab', S.prTab, 'Component rules', 'fa-solid fa-cubes');
    attachVal(d.ee, this.essIssues(S.essEntryId || S.ess[0].id, null), 'eeTab', S.eeTab, 'Definition', 'fa-solid fa-flask-vial');
    attachVal(d.ep, this.essIssues(S.essProfId || S.ess[0].id, S.sysId), 'epTab', S.epTab, 'Essence rules', 'fa-solid fa-flask-vial');
    return {d};
  }
}

</script>


</body></html>
